45b1 'grus chen po brtul ba yin ni rdo rje gar ma'o || brtson 'grus che ba ni dge ba la spro ba ste | sdig pa la sogs pa la spro ba ni dam pa'i phyir na le lo nyid de brtson 'grus ma yin no || de'i brtul ba ni 'jig rten dang 'jig rten las 'das pa thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba las yongs su rdzogs 45b2 par byed pa'o ||
de'i phyir gsang ba'i mchod pa bzhi'i bdag nyid go rims bzhin du me long lta bu'i ye shes la sogs pa las grub par rang gi rnam par thar pa'i sgo rnam par dag pa'i stobs kyis lhun gyis grub pa mngon par dga' ba'i khyad par du gyur par byed pa'i phyir ro || de nyid shes 45b3 rab 'chal pa dang | rnam par g-yeng ba dang |
yang dag pa'i smon lam med pa dang | nyam chung ba rnams dang | mi mthun pa'i phyogs zil gyis gnon pa la dga' ba nyid kyis gsungs pa | bsam gtan chen po ting 'dzin gnas || shes rab chen po lus 'chang ba || stobs po che 45b4 la thabs che ba || smon lam ye shes rgya mtsho che || zhes pa'o ||
de la tshigs bcad 'di la ni bsam gtan dang shes rab gnyis bsnor bar shes par bya'o || de'i phyir na don 'di lta ste | shes rab chen po lus 'chang ba ni rdo rje bdug pa ma'o || shes rab chen po ni shes rab chen po ste | 45b5 de nyid kyi lus de 'dzin pas na shes rab chen po lus 'chang ba'o ||
bsam gtan chen po ting 'dzin gnas ni rdo rje me tog ma'o || ting nge 'dzin ni sems rtse gcig pa'i mtshan nyid do || de la brten pas na ting 'dzin gnas te | che ba yang de yin la bsam gtan yang de nyid yin 45b6 pas na bsam gtan chen po'o || chen po ni bsam gtan bzhi'i bdag nyid kyi phyir te |
bsam gtan bzhi po de rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa'o || smon lam ni rdo rje mar me'o || smon par byed pas na smon lam ste | byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni smon lam gyi dbang gis 45b7 skye ba'o || thabs che ba ni rdo rje dri chab ma'o || chen po yang de yin la thabs kyang de yin pas thabs che ba ste |
thabs nyid dbang gis ma lus shing mi lus pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi re ba dang bsam pa ji lta ba yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i phyir ro || de nyid ni shes rab dang |
46a1 || bsam gtan dang | smon lam dang | thabs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rnams kyi mtshan nyid kyis phyi'i mchod pa'i bdag nyid bzhi rnams so || longs spyod dang bcas pa'i lam la dga' ba'i rgyu'i dngos pos stobs dang | ye shes gnyis kyi chos kyi dbyings la 46a2 'jug pa'i rang bzhin gyi phyir | stobs dang ye shes de dag rab tu rnam par dbye ba ste |
de la stobs ni dad pa la sogs pa lnga'o || ye shes ni mthong ba'i lam dang bsgom pa'i lam ste | de la mthong ba'i lam ni byang chub kyi yan lag bdun no || bsgom pa'i lam ni 'phags pa'i lam yan 46a3 lag brgyad do || de dag kyang dad pa dang | brtson 'grus dang | dran pa dang | ting nge 'dzin gyi ngo bo nyid sgo srung bzhi'i bdag nyid de |
theg pa chen po la mngon par yid ches pa dang | 'jug pa dang | de rgyun mi 'chad pa dang | yang dag par mgu bar byed pa nyid kyi phyir ro || de nyid 46a4 shes rab yongs su dag pa tsam la ltos pa'i stong pa nyid kyi rab tu dbye ba ji skad bstan pa'i sgo nas byams pa la sogs bcu drug gi ting nge 'dzin gyi bdag nyid do || de'i phyir gsungs pa |
byams chen rang bzhin dpag tu med || snying rje chen po blo yi mchog | shes rab chen po blo chen ldan || 46a5 mkhas pa chen po thabs che ba || zhes pa'o || de la byams chen rang bzhin dpag tu med ni byams pa ste | 'di la mdza' ba yod pa ni mdza' pa can te | mjug ni de'i rkyen dang ldan pa'o ||
de ni sems can thams cad la mdza' ba dang ldan pa'i rang bzhin te | che ba yang de yin la byams 46a6 pa'i rang bzhin yang de nyid yin pas byams chen rang bzhin no || de nyid kyi phyir na dpag tu med pa ste | 'jig rten gyi khams kun la bu gcig ba bzhin du byams pa nyid kyi phyir ro || snying rje chen po blo yi mchog ni 'jam dpal lo ||
snying rje chen po ni mi dmigs pa'i bdag nyid do || de gang 46a7 la yod pa de ni snying rje chen po can no || blo'i mchog ni shin tu rnam par dag pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyi ye shes yin no || des ni 'di skad gsungs par 'gyur te | zab cing rgya che ba'i bdag nyid de bcom ldan 'das yin no zhes so ||
shes rab chen po blo chen ldan ni spos kyi glang po ste | shes
46b1 rab chen po ste chos rnam par 'byed pa'i bdag nyid do || 'di dang gang ldan pa de ni shes rab can te | chen po yang de yin la shes rab kyang de yin pas shes rab chen po nyid blo chen no || mkhas pa chen po thabs chab ni ye shes tog go || gang zhig thabs che ba yin pa de ni thabs che 46b2 pa'o; |
de la mkhas pa chen po ni blo rdzogs pa chen po'o || sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang bag la nyal ba shes pas dam pa'i chos ston par byed pa nyid kyi phyir ro || rdzu 'phrul chen po stobs dang ldan || shugs chen mgyogs pa chen po ste || rdzu 'phrul chen po cher grags 46b3 pa; stobs chen pha rol gnon pa po ||
zhes de la rdzu 'phrul chen po stobs dang ldan ni bzang skyong ngo || rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs ni rdzu 'phrul stobs te | de dang ldan pa ni rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs dang ldan pa'o || chen po yang de yin la rdzu 'phrul gyi stobs dang ldan pa yang de nyid yin pas rdzu 'phrul chen po'i 46b4 stobs dang ldan pa'o ||
shugs chen mgyogs pa chen po ste blo gros rgya mtsho'o || shugs chen pos 'gro ba gang yin pa de ni shugs chen te | de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la mchod pa dang sri zhu'i don du du ma'i 'jig rten du bskyod pa'i phyir ro || de nyid kyi phyir na mgyogs pa chen po zhes 46b5 gsungs so || rdzu 'phrul chen po cher grags pa ni blo gros mi zad pa ste |
'jig rten dang nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs dang ldan pa'i phyir na rdzu 'phrul chen po'o || de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs pa'i phyir na 46b6 cher grags so || stobs chen pha rol gnon pa po ni spobs pa brtsegs pa'o ||
chen po yang de yin la stobs kyang de nyid yin pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu gnas dang gnas ma yin pa mkhyen pa la sogs pa phyis 'byung ba rnams gzung ngo || de rnams kyi pha rol gnon pa ni 46b7 de'i brtson 'grus brtsams pa dang gang ldan pa de stobs chen pha rol gnon pa po'o || srid pa'i ri bo chen po 'joms ||
mkhregs chen rdo rje chen po 'chang || drag po chen po drag shul che || 'jigs chen 'jigs par byed pa po || zhes pa ni | de la srid pa'i ri bo chen po 'joms pa ni mthu chen
47a1 || po thob pa'o || srid pa ni nye bar len pa'i phung po lnga'o || de nyid ri bo ste | mi shes pa'i brag nyid kyi phyir ro || de dag yang dag par phyin ci ma log pa'i ye shes thob pas 'joms pa'i tshul dang gang ldan pa de ni 'joms pa'o || chen po yang de yin la srid pa'i 47a2 ri bo 'joms pa yang de nyid yin pas |
srid pa'i ri bo chen po 'joms || zhes so || mkhregs chen rdo rje chen po 'chang ba ni ngan song kun 'dren no || rdo rje chen po ni kun du bzang po'i ye shes te | de 'dzin pas na rdo rje ni chen po 'chang ngo || sra ba'i phyir dang lus dang ldan pa'i phyir na mkhregs pa'o ||
47a3 drag po chen po drag shul che ba ni mya ngan gyi mun pa kun 'joms pa'i blo gros so || chen po yang yin la drag po yang de nyid yin pas na drag po chen po ste | gdul dka' ba'i sems can rnams la khro bo chen po'i gzugs ston pa'i phyir ro ||
drag shul che ni bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal de zhi ba yin yang 47a4 sems can gdul ba'i phyir drag shul can du yang ston zhes pa'i don to || 'jigs chen 'jigs par byed pa po ni dra ba can gyi 'od do || 'jigs chen ni 'jigs byed chen po'i gzugs 'dzin pas na 'jigs chen no || de yang 'jigs par byed pas na 'jigs chen 'jigs par byed pa'o ||
mgon 47a5 po rigs mchog chen po ste || bla ma gsang sngags che ba'i mchog | theg pa chen po'i tshul la gnas || theg pa chen po'i tshul gyi mchog | ces pa ni | de la mgon po rigs mchog chen po ni zla 'od gzhon nu'o || chen po yang de yin la rigs kyang de nyid yin pas rigs mchog chen po ste | gzungs 47a6 bzhi'o || de yang chig dang |
don dang sngags dang | phyag rgya'i bdag nyid rnams so || de nyid ni mgon po ste | sems can thams cad kyi lus ngag yid la kun du spyod pa'i sdig pa ma lus par yang dag par sbyong ba'i phyir ro || bla ma gsang sngags che ba'i mchog ni 'od dpag tu med pa'o ||
chen 47a7 po yang de nyid yin la gsang sngags kyang de nyid yin pas gsang sngags che ba ste | phyag rgya chen po'i bdag nyid do || de'i mchog ni gtso bo zhes pa'i don to || de nyid bla ma ste | sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang bag la nyal ji lta ba bzhin du shes nas mngon par rtogs pa nye bar ston
47b1 pa'i phyir ro || theg pa chen po'i tshul la gnas ni nam mkha'i mdzod do || theg pa chen po ni 'og nas gsungs pa ste | de'i tshul ni lam mo || de zhon pa ni gnas pa'i don to || theg pa chen po'i tshul gyi mchog ni sgrib pa rnam par sel pa'o ||
'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa gnyis brjod du 47b2 med pa'i chos nyid du khong du chud pa'i rtog pa med pa'i ye shes ni theg pa chen po zhes so || yang na 'khor ba'i gnas thabs dang spyod yul dang rten gyi rang bzhin te | bla na med pa rnam pa gsum gyis theg pa chen po bsdus pa yin zhes so ||
gal te de ltar yin na ni de'i chen po ji ltar yin zhe na | gang 47b3 dag 'jig rten thams cad la phan par byed pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das des de che ba nyid de | de rnams kyi theg pa 'di ni bskyed pa'i don dam nges par 'byung ba'i don du sgrub pa'o || de'i phyir na de dang lhan cig pa'i chen po'i theg pa yin pas theg pa chen po 'am |
chen po nyid kyi theg pa 47b4 pas theg pa chen po'o || yang na chen po rnam pa bdun gyi phyir te | dmigs pa dang | sgrub pa dang | ye shes dang | brtson 'grus dang | thabs dang | yang dag par grub pa dang | las che ba nyid kyi phyir theg pa chen po'o || chen po yang de yin la theg pa yang de nyid yin pas theg pa chen po 47b5 ste;
theg pa gzhan la de rnams med pa'i phyir ro || de bas na de mdor bsdus nas theg pa chen po ni rnam pa gsum ste | rgyu dang yul dang | rten gyi rang bzhin rnams yongs su snang ba'i don du rig par bya'o || de nyid thabs tsam la ltos pas sam |
ma lus pa'i 'jig rten dang 'jig rten 47b6 las 'das pa'i rang bzhin gyi phyir bcom ldan 'das 'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi sku'o || 'di ltar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das || rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas aa las byung ||
zhes pa nyid la sogs pa mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge tshigs bcad gzhung tshig gi 47b7 bye brag gi khyad par can 'brel tshig med pa rnams kyis khyad par du byas pa'i ye shes sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi thugs la gnas pa 'jam dpal ye shes sems dpa' 'jam dpal dpal dang ldan pa'i mchog ces bya bar blta'o ||
dkyil 'khor gyi lha sems ma rdo rje ma la
48a1 || sogs pa de rnams kyang phyag rgya chen por sbyor bas rang rang gi sku mdog dang phyag mtshan du ldan pa phyung ste | phyung nas kyang 'jig rten gyi khams kun gyi sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa byas te | yang phyir log nas mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge rnams kyi rang rang 48a2 gi rigs kyi mdog dang ldan par bsgyur te |
ji ltar don gyi rim pas rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po lngar bkod pa'i zla ba'i dkyil 'khor rnams la bzhugs par bsgoms nas | 'khor lo de'i lte ba la gnas pa de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi ye shes kyi sku 'jam dpal ye shes sems 48a3 dpa'i gzugs gong nas gsungs pa bzhin du bdag nyid mos te |
bcom ldan 'das ye shes kyi sku de nyid kyi gnyis su med pa'i yang dag pa'i don dam pa'i mtshan yang dag par brjod pa mtshan gyi gtsug gi nor bu 'di rigs kyi bu'am rigs kyi bu mo gang yang rung ba sngags kyi sgo nas spyad pa spyod 48a4 pa'i byang chub sems dpas dus gsum du kha ton du byed pa'am | glegs bam du byas pa la klog cing 'don pa dang |
gtsang ba dang mi gcang bar gyur pas te | ci yang mi smra bar ji lta ba bzhin du sus kyang mi thos par mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge tshigs bcad de ltar klog pa ni ring por mi thogs 48a5 pa kho nar yon tan las brjod pa'i yon tan gyi tshogs dang | gzhan yang 'grub par 'gyur ro ||
don dam pa'i mtshan yang dag par brjod pa yang dag par 'dzin pa'i skyes bu gang zag de ni bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs shin tu bsags te | shin tu myur bar sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan 48a6 rnams la yang dag par gyur nas bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya zhing |
bskal pa mang por yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da' ba'i chos su 'gyur la | sems can thams cad la bla na med pa'i chos ston par byed la | phyogs bcur dam pa'i chos kyi rnga sgra 48a7 sgrogs pa'i chos kyi rgyal por 'gyur ro ||
rdo rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po de'i mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig rnams gcig tu bsdu ba byas nas mtshan brgyad cu rtsa drug rnams yin te | de rnams kyang dkyil 'khor chen po'i lha rnams la rnam par phye nas sbyar bar bya'o || de la bzhi ni ye
48b1 shes sems dpa' dang zla ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi'o || yi ge aa ni gsum mo || rdo rje sems ma la sogs pa dang | rtag pa la sogs pa rnams kyi bcu gcig go || dang po rdo rje sems dpa' nas brtsams te |
ji tsam sgrib pa thams cad rnam par sel ba'i mthar thug gi bar du ma lus pa'i 48b2 mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig gi khyad par dang khyad par can gyi ngo bos go rims bzhin du sbyar bar bya'o || tshigs bcad ni bcu bzhi ste | mtshan gyi sngags kyi yi ge'i tshig rnams ni brgyad cu rtsa drug ste | de ni man ngag yin no ||
'phags pa mtshan yang dag par brjod pa'i rgya cher 'grel pa mtshan gsang 48b3 sngags kyi don du rnam par lta ba zhes bya ba las byang chub kyi sems rdo rje rje dbyings kyi dkyil 'khor chen po'i rim pa'i dbang du byas pa ste lnga pa'o || ||
[306] adhunā dvitīyaṃ vairocanasya cakram ucyate || tataś ca mañjuśrījñānasattvasya sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasya vajradhātumahāmaṇḍaladevatāvinyāsakrameṇa māhātmyam udbhāvya idānīṃ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānasya mahāvairocanadvāreṇa sarvabhāvasvabhāvavyāptiṃ pradarśayitukāmaḥ svavimokṣamukhena mohacaritānāṃ sattvānāṃ vinayanāya svacakravinyāsam āha ||
tatra ca vairocanasya mahāvairocanasya ca kiṃ nānākaraṇam iti na jñāyate tad ucyate | svābhāvikarūpakāyanaiṣyandikāśrayaparāvṛttilakṣaṇaś ca akaniṣṭhadevarājabhuvane |
tatra devanikāye copapannānāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ dharmasambhogapratyanubhavanārtham abhisambodhisamādhikrameṇa vaipākikaṃ kāyaṃ nivartya pariśuddhajñānapratibhāsalakṣaṇo naiṣyandikaḥ kāyas tatraiva bhavane mahābodhisattvagaṇaparivṛto yaḥ pradarśito 'sau bhagavān vairocanaḥ ||
yathoktaṃ śrīguhyendutilakatantre || akaniṣṭhabhuvane ramye śuddhāvāsoparisthite | tatra buddhyanti sambuddhā nirmitas tv iha buddhyata iti gāthā ||
mahāvairocanas tu jñānamayaḥ kāyaḥ kleśajñeyāvaraṇātyantavinirmuktaḥ sarvabhāvasvabhāvapratibhāsandarśanasamarthaḥ |
katham asau bhagavān tattvasvarūpād eva sarvabhāvapradarśanasamartho bhavatīti || tathā coktaṃ śrīvairocanābhisambhodhitantre || bhagavantas tathāgatā arhantaḥ samyaksambuddhāḥ sarvajñajñānaṃ prāpya tat sarvajñajñānaṃ sarvasattvebhyo vibhajya nānānayair nānābhiprāyair nānopāyanayair dharmaṃ deśayanti sma ||
keṣāṃcit śrāvakayānanayaṃ , keṣāṃcit pratyekabuddhayānanayaṃ | keṣāṃcit mahāyānanayaṃ | keṣāṃcit pañcābhijñajñānanayaṃ | keṣāṃcid devopapattaye | keṣāṃcit manuṣyopapattaye | yāvat mahoragayakṣarākṣasāsuragandharvagaruḍakiṃnarādyutpattaye dharmaṃ deśayanti sma || tatra kecit sattvā buddhavaineyikā buddharūpeṇa paśyanti | kecic chrāvakarūpeṇa |
kecit pratyekabuddharūpeṇa | kecid bodhisattvarūpeṇa | kecit maheśvararūpeṇa | kecid brahmarūpeṇa | kecin nārāyaṇarūpeṇa paśyanti sma || kecid vaiśravaṇarūpeṇa | yāvat mahoragamanuṣyāmanuṣyarūpeṇa paśyanti || svakasvakair vacanodāharaṇanayair vividheryāpathavyavasthitaṃ | tac ca sarvajñajñānam ekarasaṃ | yaduta tathatāvinirmuktarasam ity
ata āha mahāvairocana iti | virocyate dīpyata iti virocana[ḥ], virocana eva vairocana[ḥ] | svārthe vṛddhividhānāt | mahāṃś cāsau vairocanaś ca mahāvairocanaḥ | buddha iti yathāvasthitasakalapadārthāvabodhanāt | kāyavākcittamaunayogāt maunaṃ | mahac ca maunaṃ ca mahāmaunam | mahāmaunaṃ kutaḥ | satatasamāhitatvāt || tathā cāha ||
satatasamāhitā hi buddhā bhagavanta iti vacanāt | kāyādigatadauṣṭhulyābhāvāc ca tad asyāstīti mahāmaunī | mahāntaś ca te munayaś ca mahāmunayo vyāsavaśiṣṭhavālmīkaprabhṛtayaḥ || tadvineyānāṃ tathārūpeṇa pratibhāsanāt | mahāmunir ity ucyate ||
mahāmantranayodbhūta iti | mantranayo vajramahāyānaṃ tasmāt mantranayād udbhavatīti mantranayodbhūtaḥ | mahāṃś cāsau [mantranayodbhūtaś ca] mantranayodbhūtaḥ | tata eva niṣpatteḥ | ata eva mahāmantranayātmaka iti | vajramahāyānasvabhāva ity arthaḥ | tataś ca mahāvairocana iti | evamādibhiś ca bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ śrīmatāṃ vara iti vācyaḥ ||
daśapāramitāprāpto daśapāramitāśrayaḥ | daśapāramitāśuddhir daśapāramitānaya iti || (= MNS 6.2) tatra pāramitānāṃ prāptyāśrayaśuddhimārgamukhena darśayann āha | daśapāramitāprāpta iti | tatra pāramitā daśeti | tathā coktaṃ madhyāntavibhāge || dānaṃ śīlaṃ kṣamā vīryaṃ dhyānaṃ prajñā upāyatā | praṇidhānaṃ balaṃ jñānam etāḥ pāramitā daśeti |
tatra dānaṃ trividham | dharmadānam āmiṣadānaṃ maitrīdānaṃ ca || śīlaṃ trividham | samvaraśīlaṃ kuśaladharmasaṃgrāhakaśīlaṃ sattvārthakriyāśīlaṃ ca || kṣāntiḥ trividhā | dharmanidhyānakṣāntir duḥkhādhivāsanākṣāntiḥ parāpakāramarṣaṇākṣāntiś ca || vīryaṃ trividham | sannāhavīryaṃ prayogavīryaṃ pariniṣṭhāvīryaṃ ca || dhyānaṃ trividham |
doṣāpakarṣakaṃ vaihārikaṃ vaibhūtikaṃ ca || prajñā trividhā | śrutamayī cintāmayī bhāvanāmayī || upāyas trividhaḥ | svadoṣāpakarṣaṇaḥ sattvāvatārakaḥ kṣiprābhisambodhakaś ca || praṇidhis trividhaḥ | svasambhāraprābandhikaḥ sattvārthānubandhiko buddhakṣetrapariśodhanaś ca || balaṃ trividham |
karmavyāvarttakaṃ kleśāpakarṣakaṃ māraparapravādivyāvarttanaṃ ca || jñānaṃ trividham | avikalpakaṃ vikalpasamatāvabodhakaṃ sarvopāyāparokṣaṃ ca || ete dānādayaḥ sambodhicittaparipūrakāḥ sarvasattveṣu maitryāśrayaparibhāvitāḥ pāramitānāmadheyaṃ labhante || tāḥ punar laukikyo lokottarāś ca || tatrānivāritātmādyupalambhāḥ laukikyaḥ pāramitāḥ |
lokottarāś ca skandhādiṣv anātmādyadhimokṣapravṛttās tattvādhigamapravibhāvitāś ca || ata evāha madhyamakāvatāre candrakīrtiḥ || deyapratigrāhakadātṛśūnyaṃ lokottarā pāramiteti dānaṃ | nirucyate tatra yat saṅgajātaṃ tal laukikī pāramiteti nirdiṣṭam ||
śrutacintābhāvanāmayajñānodayakrameṇa sarvākārajñatādhigamaprakarṣaparyantam etīti vigṛhya kvipi sarvāpahārilope 'nityam āgamaśāsanam ity atuki | tatpuruṣe kṛti bahulam ity aluki karmavibhakteḥ kṛte | pāramir iti bhavati tadbhāvaḥ pāramitā | dānasya pāramitā dānapāramitā | evaṃ śīlādiṣv api yojyam ||
tataś ca praviṣṭabhūmikānāṃ bodhisattvānāṃ trikoṭipariśuddhilakṣaṇaṃ yad dānaṃ tad dānapāramitā | yat tu pṛthagjanānāṃ dānaṃ tad dānam eva na dānapāramitā trikoṭipariśuddhajñānavirahāt | tataś ca dānādīnāṃ pāramitānāṃ prajñāpāramitaiva cakṣur veditavyā || ata evāha madhyamakāvatāre candrakīrtiḥ |
ekena puṃsākṣimatā yatheṣṭaṃ deśaṃ samasto 'ndhagaṇaḥ sukhena | ākṛṣyate tadvad ihākṣihīnān ādāya dhīr yāti guṇāñ jinatvam iti || tad evaṃbhūtā daśapāramitāḥ prāpto 'dhigato yo bhagavān sa tathoktaḥ ||
[*daśapāramitāśraya iti |] tatrāśraya ādhāraḥ | tāsām eva daśānāṃ pāramitānām ādhārarūpatvāt tadādheyatvāc ca | tad etad uktaṃ bhavati | anyonyādhārādheyasaṃbandhāt pratītyasamutpādārthaḥ sūcito bhavatīti ||
daśapāramitāśuddhir iti | trikoṭipariśuddhatvād deyadāyakapratigrāhakānupalabdhir āsāṃ pāramitānām iti ||
daśapāramitānaya iti | daśaiva pāramitā nayo mārga upāyo yasyāsau sa tathoktaḥ || tāḥ punar bhūmiṣu yojyāḥ | tad yathā pramuditāyāṃ bhuvi dānapāramitātiriktataro bhavati | vimalāyāṃ śīlapāramitā | prabhākaryāṃ kṣāntipāramitā | arciṣmatyāṃ vīryapāramitā | sudurjayāyāṃ dhyānapāramitā | abhimukhyāṃ prajñāpāramitā | dūraṅgamāyām upāyapāramitā |
acalāyāṃ praṇidhipāramitā | sādhumatyāṃ balapāramitā | dharmameghāyāṃ jñānapāramiteti ||
tatraitā bhūmayo dharmanairātmyamaitryadhigamāviśeṣe 'pi dānapāramitādiviśuddhyadhiṣṭhānato viśeṣākhyāṃ pratilabhante || audāryaṃ tu praṇidhānabalād āsām anantam asādhāraṇaṃ ca sarvaśrāvakādibhir iti || tatra bhūmir iti jñānasyāvasthā bhūmir gocara iti yāvat |
tatra bodhyadhigamaviśeṣād uttarottarajñānapratiṣṭhāyogena tattajjñānaguṇādhārayogena ca bhūmaya ity ucyante || sarvatra sattvadhātoḥ sāmānyalakṣaṇatvād yato dharmadhātuvinirmukto na kaś cid dharmo 'stīti tata ātmasamatāṃ pratilabhante nairātmyāviśeṣāt | ekam api taj jñānaṃ daśety ucyate | avasthāviśeṣāt || ata āha ||
daśabhūmīśvaro nātho daśabhūmipratiṣṭhita iti || (MNS 44ab = 3.6ab) daśānāṃ pramuditādīnāṃ bhūmīnām īśvaro 'dhipatir ity arthaḥ | sa yasyāṃ yasyāṃ bhūmau dānādipāramitānāṃ vaśitvalābhāt nātha ity ucyate | tāsv eva daśabhūmiṣu pratiṣṭhānāt pratiṣṭhita iti ||
daśa jñānāni dharmajñānam anvayajñānaṃ paracittajñānaṃ saṃvṛtijñānaṃ duḥkhajñānaṃ samudayajñānaṃ nirodhajñānaṃ mārgajñānaṃ kṣayajñānam anutpādajñānaṃ ceti ||
dharmajñānaṃ katamat | kāmapratisaṃyukteṣu saṃskāreṣu yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | kāmapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ hetau yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | kāmapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ nirodhe yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | kāmapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ prahāṇāya mārge yad anāsravaṃ jñānam ||
api ca dharmajñāne dharmajñānabhūmau ca yad anāsravaṃ jñānam idam ucyate dharmajñānam || anvayajñānaṃ katamat | rūpārūpyapratisaṃyukteṣu saṃskāreṣu yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | rūpārūpyapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ hetau yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | rūpārūpyapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ nirodhe yad anāsravaṃ jñānam |
rūpārūpyapratisaṃyuktānāṃ saṃskārāṇāṃ prahāṇāya mārge yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | api ca khalv anvayajñāne anvayajñānabhūmau ca yad anāsravaṃ jñānam idam ucyate 'nvayajñānam || paracittajñānaṃ katamat | yaj jñānaṃ bhāvanāmayaṃ bhāvanāphalam | bhāvanāmārgasya pratilabdham avihīnam |
yena jñānena kāmāvacarān rūpāvacarān samavahitān saṃmukhībhūtān pareṣāṃ cittacaitasikān dharmāñ jānāti | ekatyāṃś cānāsravān dharmān idam ucyate paracittajñānam || saṃvṛtijñānaṃ katamat | sāsravā prajñā || duḥkhajñānaṃ katamat |
pañcopādānaskandhān anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato 'nātmataś ca manasikurvato yad anāsravaṃ jñānam idam ucyate duḥkhajñānam || samudayajñānaṃ katamat | sāsravaṃ hetuṃ hetutaḥ samudayataḥ prabhavataḥ pratyayataś ca manasikurvato yad anāsravaṃ jñānam | tat samudayajñānam || nirodhajñānaṃ katamat |
nirodhaṃ nirodhataḥ śāntataḥ praṇītato niḥśaraṇataś ca manasikurvato yad anāsravaṃ jñānam idam ucyate nirodhajñānam || mārgajñānaṃ katamat | mārgaṃ mārgato nyāyataḥ pratipattito nairyāṇikataś ca manasikurvato yad anāsravaṃ jñānam idam ucyate mārgajñānam || kṣayajñānaṃ katamat | duḥkhaṃ me parijñātam iti jānāti samudayo me prahīṇaḥ |
nirodhaḥ sākṣātkṛto mārgo me bhāvita iti jānāti tad upādāya jñānaṃ darśaṇaṃ vidyā bodhiḥ prajñāloko 'bhisamayaḥ | idam ucyate kṣayajñānam || anutpādajñānaṃ katamat | duḥkhaṃ me parijñātaṃ na punaḥ parijñeyam iti jānāti samudayaḥ prahāṇo na punaḥ prahātavyaḥ | nirodhaḥ sākṣātkṛto na punaḥ sākṣātkartavyaḥ |
mārgo me bhāvito na punar bhāvayitavya iti jānāti || tad upādāya jñānaṃ darśaṇaṃ vidyā bodhiḥ prajñāloko 'bhisamaya idam ucyate 'nutpādajñānam ity ata āha || daśajñānaviśuddhātmā daśajñānaviśuddhadhṛg iti || (MNS 44cd = 6.3cd)
tataś ca ebhir daśajñānair viśuddha ātmā svabhāvo yasya sa daśajñānaviśuddhātmā bhagavān teṣām eva daśānāṃ jñānānāṃ viśuddhiṃ prakṛtiprabhāsvaraṃ jñānaṃ dhārayatīti daśajñānaviśuddhadhṛk | sarvanimittāpagamāt ||
tathā coktaṃ Madhyāntavibhāge | ekahetutvabhoktṛtvakartṛtvavaśavartiṣu | ādhipatyārthanityārthakleśaśuddhāśrayāya ca | yogitvāmuktamuktatve ātmadarśanam eṣu hi || eṣa daśavidha ātmagrāhaḥ skandhādiṣu pravarttate || yaduta ekatvagrāhaḥ | hetutvagrāhaḥ | bhoktṛtvagrāhaḥ | kartṛtvagrāhaḥ | svatantratvagrāhaḥ | adhipatitvagrāhaḥ | nityatvagrāhaḥ |
saṃkliṣṭavyavadānatvagrāhaḥ | yogitvagrāhaḥ | muktatvagrāhaś ceti ||
ata āha || daśākāra iti | ākriyate vikṣipyate samādher ebhiś cittaṃ skandhādiṣv ity ākārā daśātmagrāhāḥ || ata evoktaṃ Dignāgapādaiḥ Saṅgrahe | daśabhiś cittavikṣepaiś cittaṃ vikṣiptam anyataḥ | yogyaṃ bhavati bālānāṃ nādvayajñānasādhana iti || tasmād ete daśākārā ātmagrāhā yasyāsau bhagavān tathoktaḥ | kimartham iti cet |
sarvavipakṣākāreṇāpi pratibhāsanāt ||
tatpratipakṣabhūtā daśārthās tatraiva paṭhyante || mūlalakṣaṇatattvaṃ ca aviparyāsalakṣaṇaṃ | phalahetumayaṃ tattvaṃ sūkṣmaudārikam eva ca || prasiddhaṃ śuddhiviṣayaṃ saṃgrāhyaṃ bhedalakṣaṇaṃ | kauśalyatattvaṃ daśadhā ātmadṛṣṭivipakṣata iti | ity evaṃ daśavidhaṃ tattvaṃ ||
yaduta mūlatattvaṃ lakṣaṇatattvam aviparyāsatattvaṃ phalahetutattvam audārikasūkṣmatattvaṃ prasiddhatattvaṃ viśuddhigocaratattvaṃ saṃgrāhakatattvaṃ bhedatattvaṃ kauśalyatattvaṃ ca || tat punar daśavidhaṃ daśavidhātmagrāhapratipakṣeṇa veditavyaṃ | ata āha || daśārthārtha iti ||
tatraiṣāṃ daśānāṃ kauśalyārthānāṃ tatpratipakṣodayabhūtānām arthatattvaṃ | arthyata ity arthaḥ | paramārthasvabhāvatvād atas teṣāṃ daśārthānām artho daśa kauśalyatattvam ātmadṛṣṭipratipakṣato veditavyam iti || tatraiva vistareṇoktam iha tu granthavistarabhayān na vivakṣitaṃ | tataś ca kim uktaṃ bhavati |
sarvavipakṣapratipakṣasvabhāvena sa eva bhagavān veditavya iti ||
tatrāviparītapratītyasamutpādopadeśataḥ saṃbhāvitāśeṣapuṇyajñānasaṃbhāraiśvaryasamṛddhair niruttarasya śāstuḥ śrāvakapratyekabuddhamunīnām adhipater munīndratvasadurlabham (!) ity ata āha || munīndra iti || ata evoktam ācāryaNāgārjunapādaiḥ || namas tasmai munīndrāya pratītyotpādavādina ityādi ||
daśa tathāgatabalāni | tadyathā sthānāsthānajñānabalaṃ | karmavipākajñānabalaṃ | nānādhātujñānabalaṃ | nānādhimuktijñānabalaṃ | indriyaparāmarṣajñānabalaṃ | nānāsamādhisamāpattijñānabalaṃ | sarvatragāminīpratipajjñānabalaṃ | cyutyupapattijñānabalaṃ | pūrvanivāsānusmṛtijñānabalaṃ | āsravakṣayajñānabalam iti ||
idaṃ ca daśajñānabalaṃ bhagavatsvabhāvasya balaviśeṣaprabhāvitatvāt | avikalpasamatayā sthānādiprapañcādhigamād ity ata āha || daśabala iti | tāni ca daśatathāgatabalāni vidyante yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
ata eva vibhur iti | māhātmyaṃ sthiracalasarvabhāvasvabhāvatvāt |
aśeṣaviśvārtha iti | aśeṣasya niravaśeṣasya viśvasya kṛtsnasya jagato 'arthaḥ | aśeṣaviśvārthas taṃ kartuṃ śīlaṃ yasyāsāv aśeṣaviśvārthakaro bhagavān ||
daśākāravaśī mahān iti | tatra daśākārā daśa vaśitāḥ | tadyathā āyurvaśitā karmavaśitā pariṣkāravaśitā vimuktivaśitā praṇidhivaśitā ṛddhivaśitā upapattivaśitā dharmavaśitā cittavaśitā jñānavaśiteti || tā daśākāravaśitālakṣaṇān dharmān vaśavartayituṃ (!) śīlaṃ yasyāsau daśākāravaśī | ataś ca sarvavaśitādharmaprāptatvād eva mahān ity ucyate ||
anādir iti | na vidyate ādir utpattir yasyāsāv anādiḥ || utpādābhāvāt vināśo nāstīty arthād gamyate || apratiṣṭhitajñānalābhāt | ādinidhane na sta ity arthaḥ ||
niṣprapañcātmeti | prapañcāḥ skandhadhātvāyatanagrāhyagrāhakalakṣaṇā dharmās teṣām ātmā svarūpaṃ nirgataḥ prapañcātmā yasmād asau niṣprapañcātmā | ata eva śuddhātmeti | āgantukamalāpagamāt || na punar asya svabhāvānyathātvaṃ | ata eva tathatātmaka iti || tatra tathatā | ananyathātvena tathatā | nityaṃ tathaiveti kṛtvā |
saivātmā svabhāvo yasya sa tathatātmakaḥ śūnyatāsvabhāva ity arthaḥ ||
bhūtavādī iti | tatra bhūtāni caturāryasatyāni | duḥkhasamudayanirodhamārgākhyaṃ | tāni vadituṃ gadituṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa tathā || atha vā gambhīrapratītyasamutpādārtho yathārthaḥ | taṃ vaditum iti pūrvavat || ata evoktam āryaNāgārjunapādaiḥ || nāpaneyam ataḥ kiṃcit prakṣeptavyaṃ na kiṃcana | draṣṭavyaṃ bhūtato bhūtaṃ bhūtadarśī vimucyata iti ||
yathāvādī tathākārīti | yathaiva sarvadharmāḥ śunyā anātmāno māyāsvapnamarīcigandharvanagarodakacandropamā iti vadati | tathaiva manasikaraṇāt yathāvādī tathākārīty ucyate || ata eva ananyavāg iti | satyavāk sarvasattvānāṃ yathāgotratayā aviparītadharmacakrapravartanāt |
advaya iti | dvayasya grāhyagrāhakasyābhāvaḥ | tasyābhāvasya bhāvo 'dvaya ucyate | bhagavān paramārthato 'dvayo 'pi san saṃvṛtyā dvayavādī || bhūtakoṭivyavasthita iti | aviparyāsārthena bhūtakoṭiviparyāsasyāvastutvāt | tasyāṃ bhūtakoṭyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ | tatsvabhāvāvyatirekatvāt ||
nairātmyasiṃhanirnādīti || tatra trividhaṃ nairātmyaṃ | alakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ vilakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ svalakṣaṇanairātmyaṃ ca || nairātmyatritayam eva siṃhaḥ | nairātmyasiṃhaḥ sarvatrācchambhitatvāt (!) siṃha iva siṃhaḥ | tasya nairātmyasiṃhasya nirnādaḥ sa yasyāstīty asau nairātmyasiṃhanirnādīty ucyate ||
kutīrthyamṛgabhīkara iti | nirvāṇasarasy avataraṇārtho mārgas tīrthaṃ | tena vyavaharantīti tīrthyāḥ | kutsitāś ca te tīrthyāś ca kutīrthyāḥ | ata eva mṛgā nairātmyasiṃhanādabhīrutvāt bhiyaṃ kartuṃ śīlaṃ yasyeti bhīkaraḥ | kutīrthyamṛgānāṃ bhīkaraḥ | tathā coktaṃ || śūnyatāsiṃhanādena trāsitāḥ sarvavādinaḥ |
yatra yatra viśanty ete tatra tatraiva śūnyatā iti ||
sarvatraga iti | tadadhiṣṭhānaprabhavatattvajñānavat | sarvatrago nirmāṇakāyas tathā prajñaptaḥ || atha vā sarvatraga iti | pañca sarvatragāḥ | tatra mahatī bhūmir eṣām iti mahābhūmikāye sarvatra cetasi bhavanti | te ca tāvat sparśamanaskāravedanāsaṃjñācetanātmakā dharmāḥ | te vidyante yasyāsau sa sarvatraga iti ||
amoghagatir iti | amoghā abandhyā gatir gamanaṃ yasyāsāv amoghagatiḥ || tathāgatamanojava iti | ekakṣaṇenaivāmeyalokadhātvatikramaṇāt || jina iti | jitā hy anenākuśalā dharmā iti jina ucyate || jitārir iti | arayaḥ kleśāḥ jitā hy aneneti jitāriḥ | tataś ca vijayīti | vijayo 'asyāstīti vijayī ||
cakravartīti | cakravartinaś catvāraḥ paṭhyante Abhidharme || teṣāṃ ca samutpattiḥ kadā kiṃcakriṇaḥ | kiṃ yugapadutpannāḥ | kiṃyāyina ity ata āha Vasubandhuḥ | cakravartisamutpattir nādho 'śītisahasrakāt | suvarṇarūpyatāmrāyaś cakriṇas te 'dharakramāt || ekadvitricaturdvīpā na ca dvau saha buddhavat |
pratyudyānasvayaṃyānakalahāstrajito 'vadhā iti || tadākāreṇa pratibhāsanāc cakravartī ||
atha vā Śrīsamvaroktavac cakravartīrūpatvāc cakravartī |
ata eva mahābala iti | mahānti ca tāni balāni ceti mahābalāni | catur aṅgāni vidyante yasya sa mahābala ity ucyate ||
gaṇamukhyo gaṇācāryo gaṇeśo gaṇapatir vaśīti | (MNS 6.8ab)
tatra gaṇamukhya iti gaṇāḥ śrāvakagaṇās teṣāṃ mukhyaḥ pradhāna ity arthaḥ | gaṇācārya iti | gaṇāḥ pratyekabuddhagaṇās teṣām aviparītopadeśadānād ācāryo mañjuśrīr eva || gaṇeśa iti | gaṇā bodhisattvagaṇās teṣām īśo gaṇeśaḥ || gaṇapatir iti | gaṇās tathāgatasamūhās teṣāṃ patiḥ svāmī bodhicittasvabhāvatvāt | ata eva vaśī |
tattatsvabhāvasaṃdarśanatayā sarvarūpaprakāśakatvāt ||
mahānubhāva dhaureyo 'nanyaneyo mahānaya iti || (MNS 6.8cd)
tatra mahānubhāva iti | anubhāvo māhātmyaṃ mahāṃś cāsāv anubhāvaś ca mahānubhāvaḥ | dhaureya iti | dharmadhuri sādhuḥ | dhaureyaḥ || ananyaneya iti | na anyaneyo 'nanyaneyo gambhīradharmābhimuktitvāt | tathā cādhimuktipradhānya (!) hi svasiddhāntāt parapravādibhir apahartuṃ na śakyata ity asāv ananyaneya iti || mahāṃś cāsau nayaś ca mahānayaḥ |
mahopāya ity arthaḥ || • ||
vāgīśo vākpatir vāgmī vācaspatir anantagīr iti | (MNS 6.9ab)
tatra vāgīśa iti | vācām īśo vāgīśaḥ | arthapratisaṃvillābhāt || vākpatir iti vācāṃ patir vākpatiḥ svāmīty arthaḥ | niruktipratisaṃvillābhāt || vācaspatir iti | pratibhānapratisaṃvillābhāt | sādhumatyāṃ bhūmau catuṣpratisaṃvillābhād dharmabhāṇaka ity ucyate || ata evāha | anantagīr iti |
anantā gīr vāg yasyāsau anantagīḥ sarvabhāṣābhiḥ sarvasattvānāṃ yathābhavyatayā dharmacakrapravartakatvāt ||
satyavāk satyavādī ca catuḥsatyopadeśaka iti || (MNS 6.9cd)
tatra satyavāg iti | dve satye yaduta saṃvṛtisatyaṃ paramārthasatyaṃ ca | tatra saṃvṛtis tridhā | prajñaptisaṃvṛtiḥ pratipattisaṃvṛtir udbhāvanāsaṃvṛtiś ca tayā saṃvṛtisatyaṃ || paramārthasatyaṃ tu ekasmāt pariniṣpannād veditavyaṃ | sa punaḥ paramārthas trividhaḥ | arthaparamārthaḥ prāptiparamārthaḥ pratipattiparamārthaś ca |
tatrārthaparamārthas tathatā | paramasya jñānasyārtha iti kṛtvā || prāptiparamārtho nirvāṇaṃ paramo 'rtha iti kṛtvā || pratipattiparamārtho mārgaḥ paramo 'syārtha iti kṛtvā || tad etat satyadvayam āśritya yasya vāk pravartate sa satyavāg iti bhaṇyate ||
tathā coktaṃ Mūlamadhyamake āryaNāgārjunapādaiḥ || dve satye samupāśritya buddhānāṃ dharmadeśanā | lokasaṃvṛtisatyaṃ ca satyaṃ ca paramārthataḥ || ye 'nayor na vijānanti vibhāgaṃ satyayor dvayoḥ | te dharmaṃ na vijānanti gambhīre buddhaśāsana iti ||
tataś ca satyavādīti | satyaṃ bhūtaṃ śūnyatāsvabhāvam evaṃ vadituṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa tathā | catuḥsatyopadeśaka iti | sakalajñeyābhisaṃkṣepalakṣaṇāni catvāri satyāni [|] duḥkhasatyaṃ skandhādau viparyāsalakṣaṇaṃ | āryāṇāṃ pratikūlatvāt || samudayasatyaṃ samāsato yathoktaṃ duḥkham eva avidyāyāḥ saṃskārādipratyayatvena jātyādiduḥkhahetutvāt ||
nirodhasatyaṃ sarvadharmāṇāṃ yathāyaṃ (!) parikalpalakṣaṇatvād bhagavān mohapratipakṣabhūtaḥ | tadadhigamaṃ ca | mārgasatyaṃ saṃbodhiparipūraṇato maitrīprabhavas tyāgādis teṣāṃ deśakaḥ catuḥsatyopadeśakaḥ ||
avaivartiko hy anāgāmī khaḍgapratyekanāyaka iti || (MNS 6.10ab)
tatrāvaivartika iti | anuttarāyāḥ samyaksaṃbodher yo 'parāṅmukhaḥ so 'vaivartika ucyate || yathā śrāvakagotre na vivartako 'vivartakaḥ tena dīpyatīti avaivartikaḥ acalāyāṃ bhuvi vyākaraṇalābhāt || anāgāmīti śraddhādṛṣṭiprāptaḥ | tato 'yaṃ kāmāvacaranavakleśaprakāraprahāṇāt | anāgāmīty ucyate |
tathā coktaṃ | kāme 'ṣṭamaprakārasya na[*vamasya ca saṃkhyayā] [ 42-43 desunt] [ reads: kāmeṣu ṣaṭprakārasya navamasya ca saṃkhyayā | jāyate sakṛdāgāmī anāgāmī ca pudgala iti ||]
[296: dvividhaḥ pratyekabuddhaḥ khaḍgacārī vargacārī ca | tatra khaḍgacāriṇam adhikṛtya āha | khaḍga iti | khaḍga iva khaḍgaḥ | khaḍgo nāma gaṇḍakaḥ | tasya mukhe eka eva viṣāṇo bhavati | tena tulyatvād ekacārī pratyekabuddhaḥ khaḍga ity ucyate || pratyekanāyaka iti vargacārī | ekam ekaṃ prati nāyaḥ pratyekanāyakaḥ | vargacāritvāt ||]
[298: nānāniryāṇaniryāto mahābhūtaikakāraṇa iti || (MNS 6.10cd) tatra nānāniryāṇaniryāta iti | nānāniryāṇāni śrāvakādinirvāṇāni tainniścayena (!) yāto niryātaḥ | yadyadvaineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ cittasantāneṣu tattadrūpeṇa pratibhāsanāt | mahābhūtaikakāraṇa iti | tatra bhūtāni catvāri pṛthivyādīni |
nanu cākāśasyāpi mahābhūtatvāt pañca mahābhūtāni kathaṃ catvāri | ucyate mahānti ca tāni bhavanti ceti mahābhūtāni vipulākāśadeśā((ntare)) avasthānāt | mahattvam eṣāṃ pṛthivīdhātvādīnāṃ sādhāraṇāsādhāraṇakarmaviśeṣajatvāc ca bhūtatvaṃ | ato 'syākāśasya mahattvaṃ sarvarūpotpattyavakāśadānān na tu bhūtatvaṃ | asaṃskṛtatvād yato bhāvanārthe bhūḥ |
ato na mahābhūtam ākāśaṃ | tasmāc catvāry eva mahābhūtāni | teṣāṃ caturṇāṃ mahābhūtānām eko 'dvitīyaḥ kāraṇaṃ hetur yaḥ sa tathoktaḥ ||]
[294: arhat kṣīṇāsravo bhikṣur vītarāgo jitendriya iti || (MNS 6.11ab)]
bhikṣur iti | tathā cĀbhidharme catvāro bhikṣavaḥ paṭhyante || yaduta saṃjñābhikṣuḥ bhikṣaṇaśīlo bhikṣuḥ jñaptibhikṣuḥ paramārthabhikṣuś ca | teṣāṃ caturṇām ayaṃ paramārthabhikṣuḥ | bhinnakleśatvāt sarvamārair abhedyatvāc ca ||
[294: tatrārhann iti | arayaḥ kleśāḥ tān arīn hatavān ity arhat | saṃsāre punar arhaddharmatayā vā sakalaparisamāptārthatvāt | pareṣām anuśāsanaṃ kartum arhati śaknotīti vā | buddharmasaṅgharatnam arhatīti vā | agrais traidhātuke mkriyamāṇām (!?) anekavidhāṃ pūjām arhatīti vā | niruttaratathāgataṃ pratipattipūjābhir arhayati pūjayatīti vā |
arhann ucyate bhagavān ||]
[296: kṣīṇāsrava iti tatra catvāro dharmā akuśalamūlam āsravanti | utpādayantīti vā | kleśayakṣam (!?) āsravantīti vā | ābhavāgrād yāvad avīciṃ ṣaḍbhir āyatanavraṇaiḥ sattvān kliśanti bādhanta iti kleśāsravā rūpādayaḥ | trayo bhavāḥ | (?) sahajā parivikalpitā cāvidyā | dvāṣaṣṭir dṛṣṭaya iti |
kāmāsravo [bhavāsravo] 'vidyāsravo dṛṣṭyāsrava ity ucyate | aśaikṣeṇa śīlasamādhiskandhena kāmāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | aśaikṣeṇa prajñāskandhena dṛṣṭyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | aśaikṣeṇa vimuktiskandhena bhavāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | aśaikṣeṇa vimuktijñānadarśanaskandhenāvidyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | śīlaparipūribhūmyāṃ vā darśanamārge samyagdṛṣṭyā dṛṣṭyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ |
samādhiparipūribhūmyāṃ vā anāgāmimārge samyaksamādhānāt kāmāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | prajñāparipūribhūmyāṃ vārhanmārge samyagvimuktyāvidyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | [...] arhanmārge vā kāmopadhiparijñānāt kāmāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | saṃskāropadhiparijñānād avidyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | pañcopādānaskandhālambanād dhi (?) sarvadṛṣṭayaḥ |
sarvaduḥkhasatyasamudayasatyaprahāṇāt kāmāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | nirodhasatyasākṣātkaraṇād bhavāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | mārgasatyabhāvanayāvidyāsravaḥ kṣīṇaḥ | kāmāsravaḥ prahāṇād devaputramāro bhagno bhavati | bhavāsravaprahāṇān maraṇamāro bhagno bhavati | avidyāsravaprahāṇāt kleśamāro bhagno bhavati | dṛṣṭyāsravaprahāṇāt skandhamāro bhagno bhavati ||]
iti caturmāraparājayāt bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ kṣīṇāsravaḥ | ata ete āsravāḥ kṣīṇā yasyāsau sa tathā ||
vītarāga ity anenaiva ca traidhātuke caturāryasatyānāṃ ṣoḍaśākāraprakāreṇāṣṭānavatikleśānāṃ darśanabhāvanāmārgābhyāṃ prahāṇād vītarāga ity ucyate | ata eva jitendriya iti | sarvaviṣayair anabhibhavanīyatvāt ||
kṣemaprāpto 'bhayaprāptaḥ śītībhūto hy anāvila iti || (MNS 6.11cd)
tatra kṣemaprāpta ity anena kṣemaṃ kāyakarma tatprāpto yaḥ sa kṣemaprāptaḥ | bhītir bhayaṃ na vidyate bhayaṃ yasya tad abhayaṃ vākkarma sarvavāgdoṣābhāvād vākkarmaṇaḥ | tatprāpta ity abhayaprāptaḥ ||
śītībhūta ity anena śītaṃ śāntatvāt manaḥkarma sarvacittopaplavābhāvād aśītaḥ prāgrāgādibhir abhibhūtatvāt paścāt tadapagamāc chītībhavatīti śītībhūtaḥ | tata eva hīti yasmādarthe kāyavākcittadoṣābhāvād anāvila ucyate ||
vidyācaraṇasampannaḥ sugato lokavit para iti | (MNS 6.12ab)
tatra vidyācaraṇasampanna ity anena tasya jñānasattvasya śāstṛsampadaḥ prāptihetuṃ darśayati | vidyācaraṇam āryāṣṭāṅgo mārgaḥ | samyagdṛṣṭir vidyāśeṣāṇy aṅgāni caraṇaṃ | na hy apaśyann acaraṇo vā gantuṃ samartha iti kṛtvā | tisro vā śikṣā vidyācaraṇaṃ | adhiprajñā śikṣā adhiśīlam adhicittaṃ ca |
tatrādyā vidyā dve caraṇaṃ prajñāyāḥ pūrvaparikarmakartrītvāt puraścaraṇaṃ hi caraṇam iti kṛtvā vidyāyās tu pūrvagrahaṇaṃ tatpariśuddhyā śīlasamādhipariśuddhitaḥ | tathā hi prajñayā cakṣuṣaiva paśyan tābhyāṃ śīlasamādhibhyāṃ caraṇābhyām iva gacchan gantavyam anuprāpnotīti vidyācaraṇaśabdena tisraḥ śikṣā nirdiśyante ||
atas tisṛbhir vidyābhiḥ sampannā vidyācaraṇasampanna ity ucyate ||
sugata ity anena ca yathāgantavyaṃ gatas tad darśayati | suṣṭhu gataḥ sugato 'punarāvṛttyarthena sunaṣṭajvaravat || niḥśeṣaṃ gataḥ | niḥśeṣajñeyagamanārthena suparipūrṇaghaṭavat || etac cārthadvayaṃ bāhyaśrāvakādibhyo viśeṣaṇārthaṃ | bāhyā hi na suṣṭhu gatāḥ punarāvṛttitaḥ | śrāvakādayo yady api suṣṭhu gatāḥ punaḥ pṛthagjanatāyā abhavyatvāt |
na tu niḥśeṣaṃ gatāḥ sarvajñeyeṣu jñānavibandhanatvāt ||
evaṃ vidyācaraṇasampadā suṣṭhu niḥśeṣaṃ gatasya śāstṛsampadaṃ prāptasya bhagavato jñānasattvasya tacchāstṛkarma lokavit para ity ābhyāṃ padābhyāṃ darśayati | tat punaḥ śāstṛkarma dvividhaṃ bhavyābhavyalokālokanaṃ (!) | bhavyavinayanaṃ ca | tatra lokavid ity anena bhavābhavyāvalokanaṃ |
lokaṃ vettīti lokavid bhavyābhavyalokaparijñānād asau lokavid ucyate |
tathā hi bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ triṣkṛtvā rātres triṣkṛtvā divasasya prajñācakṣuṣā lokaṃ vyavalokayati | ko hīyate ko varddhate kaḥ saṃkaṭam anuprāptaḥ ko 'pāyanimnaḥ | yam ahaṃ pāpād uddhṛtya svarge mokṣe ca pratiṣṭhāpayeyaṃ kasyānavaropitāni kuśalamūlāny avaropayeyaṃ kasyāvaropitāni paripūrayeyaṃ kasyāparipakvāni pācayeyam iti |
ata eva hi para utkṛṣṭo 'sau bhagavān ||
nirmamo nirhaṅkāraḥ satyadvayanaye sthita iti | (MNS 6.12cd)
tatra nirmama ity anena padena grāhyāṃśo niṣiddhaḥ | nirahaṃkāra ity anena padena grāhakāṃśas tadubhayasyāpi mithyāpratibhāsatvāt | tathā cāryaNāgārjunapādair uktaṃ Ratnāvalyāṃ | asty ahaṃ mama cāstīti mithyaitat paramārthataḥ | yathābhūtaparijñānān na bhavaty ubhayaṃ yata iti || satyadvayanaye sthita iti gatārthaṃ ||
saṃsārapārakoṭisthaḥ kṛtakṛtyaḥ sthale sthita iti || (MNS 6.13ab)
tatra saṃsārapārakoṭistha ity anenaitad darśayati | saṃsāro hi bhāvarūpaḥ pañcopādānaskandhatvāt | ta eva hi pañcopādānaskandhāḥ kleśakarmāviddhagatayo gater gatyantaraṃ gacchantaḥ saṃsāra ity ucyate | tasya pāro nirvāṇaṃ tac cābhāvarūpaṃ | saṃsāraś ca pāraś ca saṃsārapārau tayoḥ koṭiḥ saṃsārapārakoṭis tasyāṃ tiṣṭhatīti saṃsārapārakoṭisthaḥ |
etad uktaṃ bhavati | bhāvābhāvasvabhāvayoḥ saṃvṛtyā sthito 'sau bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ na paramārthataḥ | kasmāt | tayoḥ sūkṣmasyāpi dharmasyānupalabdher iti | tathā coktaṃ | nirvāṇasya ca yā koṭiḥ koṭiḥ saṃsaraṇasya ca | na tayor antaraṃ kiṃcit susūkṣmam api vidyata iti ||
kṛtakṛtya iti | avaśyakartavyaṃ kṛtyam ity ucyate | tatra śīlasamādhiprajñāparipūryā sarvaduḥkhavimokṣapradhānakarma tatparisamāptyā kṛtakṛtya ucyate || alpecchāvṛttaṃ saṃtoṣavṛttaṃ dhūtāvṛttaṃ saṃrañjanīyavṛttam ityādayo vṛttiviśeṣāḥ pūrayitavyāḥ kṛtā anenety asau kṛtakṛtya iti || sthale sthita iti || sthalaṃ nirvāṇaṃ kṣemasthānīyatvāt |
tatra sthitaḥ sarvadā dharmakāyasthitatvād iti || advayadharmatāyām avasthānāt ||
kaivalyajñānaniṣṭhyutaḥ prajñāśastravidāraṇa iti | (MNS 6.13cd)
kevalaṃ śuddhaṃ tatra jātaṃ kaivalyaṃ kaivalyaṃ taj jñānaṃ ceti kaivalyajñānaṃ || tasmāj jāto niṣṭhyutaḥ sa tathoktaḥ || prajñāśastravidāraṇa iti | prajñā dharmapravicayalakṣaṇātmikā saiva śastraṃ sarvakleśanikṛntanatvāt | tena prajñāśastreṇa sarvadharmāṇāṃ heyopādeyaviviktāvabodhanāt | sa tathoktaḥ ||
saddharmo dharmarāḍ bhāsvān lokālokakaraḥ para iti | (MNS 6.14ab)
tatra dvividhaḥ saddharmaḥ | gauṇo mukhyaś ca | tatra satāṃ dharmaḥ saddharmaḥ | santo hi buddhā bodhisattvāś ca teṣāṃ dharmo dvādaśāṅgapravacanāni | tatra dvādaśāṅgāni | sūtraṃ geyaṃ gāthā vyākaraṇam udānaṃ nidānaṃ jātakam itivṛttakam avadānaṃ vaipulyaṃ niruktir adbhutaś cety aṅgāni ||
tāni pratyakṣair akṣaraiḥ pustakeṣu rūḍhāni gauṇaḥ saddharma ucyate || mukhyas tu dharmarāḍ iti dharmadhātusvabhāvaḥ | vācyavācakarahitatvāt ||
[326: asāv avijñeyaḥ | tathā coktaṃ Triśatikāyāṃ | dharmato buddhā draṣṭavyā dharmakāyā hi nāyakāḥ | dharmatā cāpy avijñeyā na sā śakyā vijānitum iti || ataś cāha | bhāsvān iti | ubhāv api dharmau bāhyādhyātmikāvidyātamovidhvaṃsanāt | lokālokakara iti lokasyājñānatamovṛtasya yathāvasthitadharmaprakāśatvāt | lokālokakaraḥ |
ataś ca para utkṛṣṭo 'sau bhagavān ||]
[328: dharmeśvaro dharmarājaḥ śreyomārgopadeśaka iti || (MNS 6.14cd) tatra dharmeśvara iti | dharmāṇāṃ daśakuśalakarmapathānām īśvaro dharmeśvaraḥ | tathā coktaṃ | nivṛttir aśubhāt kṛtsnāt pravṛttiś ca śubhe sadā | manasā karmaṇā vācā dharmo 'yaṃ trividhaḥ smṛta iti || dharmarājā iti | dharmasya bāhyaguhyatriyānabhedabhinnasya rājā dharmarājā |
śreyomārgopadeśaka iti | śreyaso mārgaḥ śreyomārgas tatpratipādako dharmaḥ śreyomārga ucyate | tasyopadeśakaḥ śreyomārgopadeśako bhagavān | tathā coktaṃ Ratnāvalyāṃ | naiḥśreyasaḥ punar dharmaḥ sūkṣmo gambhīradarśanaḥ | bālānām aśrutavatām uktas trāsakaro jinair iti ||]
[323: siddhārthaḥ siddhasaṃkalpaḥ sarvasaṃkalpavarjita iti || tatrārthyata ity arthaḥ | rūpādiviṣayaḥ sa ca grāhyagrāhakabhāvenātīva lokaprasiddhaḥ | siddhaś cāsāv arthaś ca siddhārthaḥ | tathā hi bahirarthatayā vikalpitabhāvānāṃ rūpādīnām ātmano jātiguṇakriyādīnāṃ vā lakṣaṇānāṃ tattvato 'bhiniveśaḥ parikalpitasvabhāvaḥ ||
tathā coktam ācāryaVasubandhupādaiḥ | yena yena vikalpena yad yad vastu vikalpyate | parikalpita evāsau svabhāvo na sa vidyata iti || siddhasaṃkalpa iti | saṃkalpyata iti saṃkalpaḥ | siddhaś cāsau saṃkalpaś ca siddhasaṃkalpaḥ paratantrasvabhāvaḥ | hetupratyayais tenbhyata (!?) iti paratantraḥ |]
tathā hi kuśalākuśalānañjasaṃskārādibahuvidhavāsanānugatasvakārakalāpāt pratikṣaṇam utpadyamānasya tattvato 'dvayasyāpi bhrāntatatayā dvayapratibhāsinaḥ cittacaittakakalpanālakṣaṇasya paratantrasya yā nityatvahetutvaviṣayatvakalpanā ((asau)) paratantraḥ | tathā cāha | paratantrasvabhāvas tu vikalpaḥ pratyayodbhava iti || sarvasaṃkalpavarjita iti |
pariniṣpannasvabhāvaḥ | etasyaiva paratantra[sya] prakṛtyā mārgaviśuddhyā vā sarvāvaraṇa[vigamāt] nirmalādvayaśūnyatā pariniṣpannasvabhāvaḥ | tathā coktaṃ | niṣpannas tasya pūrveṇa sadā rahitatā tu yeti | tataś ca trisvabhāvo bhagavān sarvatathāgatajñānasattvaḥ ||
nirvikalpo 'kṣayo dhātur dharmadhātuḥ paro 'vyaya iti | (MNS 6.15cd)
tatra nirvikalpa iti | pariniṣpannajñānavikalpanābhāvāt | tathā cāha | na vikalpād ṛte kaścit saṃsāro nāma vidyate | tenāpetavikalpasya sadaiva bhavanirvṛtir iti || akṣaya iti | śūnyatāyā ākāśatulyatvāt || dhātur iti | utpattimatāṃ sarvasaṃskārāṇāṃ heturūpatvāt | dhātvartho hy atra hetvarthaḥ || dharmadhātur iti |
āryadharmāṇāṃ tadālambanaprabhāvitatvāt || para iti | bāhyaśrāvakādibhyo viśiṣṭatvāt || avyaya iti | asaṃskṛtatvāt ||
puṇyavān puṇyasaṃbhāro jñānaṃ jñānākaraṃ mahat iti | (MNS 6.16ab)
tatra punātīti puṇyaṃ tad asyāstīti puṇyavān || puṇyasambhāra iti | samyagbharaṇaṃ saṃbhāraḥ | puṇyasya saṃbhāraḥ puṇyasaṃbhāraḥ | tataś cātra padadvayenaitad uktaṃ bhavati | laukikalokottarapuṇyasambhārair aviyukta ity arthaḥ || jñānam iti | bāhyaśrāvakādijñānaṃ | jñānāntaram iti |
bāhyaśrāvakādijñānebhyo 'nyaj jñānaṃ jñānāntaraṃ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānaṃ jñānagataṃ ca taccittam iti | bāhyasyārthasya pramāṇaviruddhatvenāsattvāt | pramāṇavirodhasya ca vistareṇa pratipāditatvāt || cittamātraṃ bho jinaputrā yaduta traidhātukam iti | āgamāt | sarvabhāvasaṃdarśanasya ca | sarvapramāṇaviruddhatvāt | jñānāntaraṃ |
ata eva mahad iti | aśeṣānavaśeṣasattvadhātuparitrāṇāśayatvāt ||
[332: jñānavān sadasajjñānī sambhāradvayasambhṛta iti | (MNS 6.16cd) jñānavān iti tad evaṃbhūtaṃ jñānam asyāstīti jñānavān | sadasajjñānīti | sac cāsac ca sadasatī tayor jñānaṃ tad asyāstīti sadasajjñānī | tathā coktam Āryadevapādaiḥ || sat sadasad asac ceti yasya pakṣo na dīyate | upālambhaś cireṇāpi tasya vaktuṃ na śakyata iti ||
anantaroktena sambhāradvayena sambhṛtaḥ paripūrṇa ity arthaḥ ||]
[335: śāśvato viśvarāḍ yogī dhyānadhyeyau dhiyāṃ patir iti | (MNS 6.17ab) tatra śāśvata iti prabandhanityatayā | āsaṃsāropasthānāt | dharmadhātusvabhāvasyānapāyitvād ity abhiprāyaḥ | viśvaṃ kṛtsnaṃ jagat | rājate dīvyata iti rāṭ | viśvasya rāḍ viśvarāṭ | sthiracalasarvabhāvātmakatvāt || yogīti |
jñānamātrasamāpattir yoga ity abhidhīyata iti vacanāt | yogo 'dvayajñānaṃ tad asyāstīti yogī | dhyānam iti caturdhyānātmakatvāt | dhyeyam iti dhyātavyaviṣayatvāt | dhiyāṃ patir iti | dhiyāṃ buddhānāṃ patiḥ svāmī | sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasvabhāvatvāt |]
[339: pratyātmavedyo hy acalaḥ paramādyas trikāyadhṛg iti | (MNS 6.17cd) tatra pratyātmavedya iti | ātma[34]nā pratipattivedyaḥ pratyātmavedyaḥ | dharmadhātujñānasya svasaṃvedyatvāt | kumārīsuratasukhavat pareṣām aprakāśayogāt | tathā coktaṃ |
yo naiko nāpy anekaḥ svaparahitamahāsampadādhārabhūto naivābhāvo na bhāvaḥ kham iva samaraso durvibhāvyasvabhāvaḥ | nirlepaṃ nirvikāraṃ śivam asamasamaṃ vyāpinaṃ niṣprapañcaṃ vande pratyātmavedyaṃ tam aham anupamaṃ dharmakāyaṃ jinānām iti ||]
[343: ata evācala iti | hīti yasmādarthe | ataś cāsau bhagavān mañjuśrīr acalaḥ sarvavikalpāgocaratvāt || paramādya iti | ādau bhava ādyaḥ paramaś cāsāv ādyaś ca paramādyaḥ | sa ca bhagavān śrīvajrasattvaḥ || trikāyadhṛg iti | trayaḥ kāyāḥ | dharmasambhoganirmāṇalakṣaṇāḥ | tān dhārayatīti trikāyadhṛk ||]
[346: pañcakāyātmako buddhaḥ pañcajñānātmako vibhuḥ | pañcabuddhātmamakuṭaḥ pañcacakṣur asaṅgadhṛg (!) iti | (MNS 6.18) tatra pañcakāyātmaka iti svābhāvikasāmbhogikavaipākikanairmāṇikadharmakāyātmakaḥ pañca kāyāḥ | ta evātmā svabhāvo yasyāsau pañcakāyātmakaḥ | buddha iti gatārthaṃ | pañcajñānātmaka iti |
ādaśasamatāpratyavekṣaṇākṛtyānuṣṭhānasuviśuddhadharmadhātujnnānam ity etāni pañca jñānāni | tāny evātmā svabhāvo yasya sa pañcajñānātmakaḥ | vibhur iti gatārthaṃ | pañcabuddhātmamukuṭa iti | pañca buddhā akṣobhyaratnasambhavāmitābhāmoghasiddhivairocanāḥ | tadātmakas tatsvabhāvo mukuṭo yasyāsau pañcabuddhātmamukuṭaḥ || pañcacakṣur iti |
māṃsadivyaprajñādharmabuddhacakṣūṃsi pañca | tāni vidyante yasya sa pañcacakṣuḥ | ata evāsaṅgadṛg (!) iti | asaṅgā asaṃsaktā sarvajñeyeṣv asuvālitā (!?) dṛg darśanaṃ yasyāsāv asaṅgadṛk | tathā coktam Abhidharme | dvitrisāhasrikāsaṃkhyā dṛg arhatkhaḍgadaiśikā iti |]
[343: janakaḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ buddhaputraḥ paro vara iti | (MNS 6.19ab) janakaḥ sarvabuddhānām iti | tatra janaka utpādakaḥ sarvabuddhānāṃ bodhicittasvabhāvatvāt | ata evoktaṃ Śrītattvasaṃgrahe | sambhūtāḥ sambhaviṣyanti tvām āsādya tathāgatā iti || buddhaputra iti |
sarvavāsanāvaraṇanirmuktaḥ sarvaprakārasarvadharmāvabodhakaḥ sarvacittasyāśrayabhūto nirvikalpajñānaviśeṣo buddhas tasya putro buddhaputraḥ | ātmajaḥ putra ucyate | tasya cātmā paramārthena tathatā tatprabhāvitatvāt | nirvikalpajñānasya tatprativepeto (!?) vā jāta iti buddhaputro bhaṇyate | anenārthena bodhisattvo buddhaputra ity ucyate ||
para iti | utkṛṣṭatvāt || vara iti abhigamanīyānāṃ madhye śreṣṭhatvāt |]
[348: prajñābhavodbhavo yonir dharmayonir bhavāntakṛd iti | (MNS 6.19cd) tatra prajñā dharmapravicayaḥ sa ca lokottaro dharmapudgalayor vivekaḥ prajñaiva bhavaḥ prajñābhavaḥ pañca śuddhaskandhāḥ śīlasamādhiprajñāvimuktivijñānadarśanātmakāḥ | tebhya udbhavatīti prajñābhavodbhava ity ucyate bhagavān ||
yonir iti | yoniḥ kāraṇam utpattimatāṃ sāsravāṇāṃ dharmāṇāṃ tadutpatteḥ || dharmayonir iti | dharmāṇāṃ yonir dharmayoniḥ | dharmā anāsravāḥ kuśalāḥ karmapathās tesāṃ yonir hetur ity arthaḥ || bhavāntakṛd iti | bhavaḥ saṃsāras tasyāntam avasānam | tat karotīti bhavāntakṛt ||
ghanaikasāro vajrātmā sadyojāto jagatpatir iti | tatra ghanaikasaara iti | ghanaś cāsāv ekasāraś ca ghanaikasāraḥ | ghano nibiḍaḥ | avidyamānāntaratvāt | sāraḥ kleśamalābhāvāt | ata eva vajrātmeti vajraṃ jñānam abhedyatvāt | tad evātmā svabhāvo yasyāsau vajrātmā sarvatathāgatānāṃ jñānakāyatvāt || sadyojāta iti |
yatra yatra sattvārthaṃ paśyati tatra tatra sadyas tatkṣaṇam eva janmaparigrahāt | sadyojāta ity ucyate | utpattivaśitālābhāt || jagatpatir iti jagatāṃ patir jagatpatiḥ || jagatsvāmīty arthaḥ | teṣāṃ sarvasampattyādhārabhūtatvāt ||
gaganodbhavaḥ svayambhūḥ prajñājñānānalo mahān iti || (MNS 6.20ab) tatra gaganodbhava iti | gaganaṃ śūnyatājñānaṃ tasmād udbhavatīti gaganodbhavaḥ || svayambhūr iti | svayaṃ bhavatīti svayambhūḥ | bodhicittavajrātmakatvāt || prajñājñānānala iti | prajñāyā advayalakṣaṇāyā jñānaṃ prajñājñānam avikalpātmakaṃ |
tad evānalaḥ savāsanakleśendhanadahanakṣamatvāt || mahān iti | kṣitijalapavanahutāśanayajamānākāśasomasūryāṣṭamūrtitvena mahān | jñānabalavīryatejaḥśaktyaiśvaryaṣaḍguṇyayogāt ||
vairocano mahādīptir jñānajyotir virocana iti || (MNS 6.21ab) tatra vairocana iti gatārthaṃ || mahādīptir iti | mahatī dīptir mahādīptiḥ | suviśuddhajñānamukhena dīptir jñeyāvabhāsasādharmyeṇa || jñānajyotir iti | grāhyagrāhakarahitatvāt advayaṃ jñānaṃ | tasya jyotir jñāna-jyotir ity ādarśajñānasvabhāvatvāt | virocyate dīvyata iti virocanaḥ |
sāmbhogikakāyena sakalajagadarthakaraṇāt ||
jagatpradīpo jñānolko mahātejāḥ prabhāsvara iti || (MNS 6.21cd) jagatpradīpa iti | jagatāṃ pradīpo jagatpradīpaḥ pratyavekṣaṇājñānasvarūpatvāt || jñānolka iti jñānasyolkevaulkādīptir yasya sa jñānolka iti | samatājñānasvabhāvena || mahātejā iti | mahat tejo 'vabhāso yasya mahātejāḥ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānamukhena || prabhāsvara iti |
prakarṣeṇa bhāsvaraḥ prabhāsvaraḥ pañcajñānātmakatvāt | samantād dīpanaśīlo bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ ||
vidyārājo 'gramantreśo mantrarājo mahārthakṛd iti || (MNS 6.22ab) tatra vidyārājā iti | vidyānāṃ rājā vidyārājaḥ sarvāsāṃ vidyānāṃ dhāraṇīmaṇḍalagatānāṃ tajjanyatād agramantreśa iti | agrāś cāsau mantraś ceti agramantraḥ | tasyeśaḥ prabhuḥ | agramantraś cātra hūṃkāro draṣṭavyaḥ |
tasya mahāmudrātmakatvān mantrarāja iti mantrāṇāṃ dharmamaṇḍalaparipaṭhitānāṃ rājā mantrarājaḥ || mahārthakṛd iti | mahāṃś cāsāv arthaś ca mahārthaḥ | taṃ karotīti mahārthakṛd iti |
karmamaṇḍaloktābhiḥ sakalalaukikalokottarapūjābhis tisṛbhir bāhyaguhyamānasikībhiś ca sarvatathāgatapūjākartṛtvenāmeyapuṇyajñānasambhāropārkajatvena ca sarvasattvārthakaraṇāt ||
mahoṣṇīṣo 'dbhutoṣṇīṣo viśvadarśī viyatpatir iti || (MNS 6.22cd) tatra mahoṣṇīṣa iti | mahāṃś cāsāv uṣṇīṣaś ca mahoṣṇīṣaḥ sarvatathāgatoṣṇīṣākāreṇa rāśībhūtvā vyavasthitatvāt | sarvatathāgatamūrdhni ataś ca mahoṣṇīṣa ity ucyate || adbhutoṣṇīṣa iti |
tejorāśimukhena yo 'sau bhagavān sarvatathāgatajñānasattvaḥ sarvatathāgatoṣṇīṣebhyo niścaritvā vajradharamūrdhni vyavasthitaḥ so 'dbhutoṣṇīṣa ucyate || viśvadarśīti | sakalalaukikalokottarasamādhisvabhāvena viśvaṃ nānāprakāraṃ samādhimukhaṃ darśituṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa viśvadarśī || viyatpatir iti | viyad ākāśaṃ tasya patiḥ viyatpatiḥ |
śūnyatāyāḥ patir ity arthaḥ || tathā coktaṃ || nirālambasya cittasya sthitir ākāśalakṣaṇā | ākāśabhāvanaivaiṣā śūnyatābhāvanā mateti ||
sarvabuddhātmabhāvāgryo jagadānandalocana iti || (MNS 6.23ab) tatra sarvabuddātmabhāvāgrya iti | sarve ca te buddhāś ca sarvabuddhāḥ śāśvatādayas teṣām ātmā sarvabuddhātmā tasya sarvabuddhātmanaḥ svabhāvasya bhāvaḥ sattā sarvabuddhātmabhāvo dharmadhātuḥ | agrabhavo 'grāḥ |
sarvabuddhātmabhāvena tathataikarasatvena agryo bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ sarvatathāgatajñānakāyatvāt tasya bhagavataḥ || jagadānandalocana iti | jagatām ānando jagadānandas tasya jagadānandasya locano bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ | yena heyopādeyapravicayātmakatvāt prajñācakṣuḥ ||
viśvarūpī vidhātā ca pūjyo mānyo mahāṛṣibhir iti || (MNS 6.23cd) tatra viśvarūpīti | sthiracalasarvabhāvasvabhāvatayā nānārūpaṃ darśakatvāt | vidhātā ceti | sarvasattvānāṃ yathābhavyatayā sarvamantramudrāsamayamaṇḍalotsargāt || sarvalaukikalokottarapūjārhatvena pūjaḥ pūjanīyaḥ | ata eva mānyo mānanīyaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ || mahāṛṣibhir iti |
mahāṃś cāsāv ṛṣiś ca mahāṛṣiḥ | buddho bhagavān mahāṛṣi ity ucyate ||
kulatrayadharo mantrī mahāsamayamantradhṛg iti | (MNS 6.24ab) tatra kulatrayaṃ kāyavākcittabhedena tan dhārayatīti kulatrayadharaḥ || mantrīti | mantrāḥ sakalalaukikalokottarāḥ te vidyante yasya sa mantrī | sarvamantramūrtidharatvād bhagavato jñānasattvasya || mahāsamayamantradhṛg iti |
mahāṃś cāsau samayaś ca mahāsamayaḥ sa ca bhagavān bodhicittavajras tasya mantraḥ | mantryate trāyata iti mantraḥ | jñānakṛpātmakatvāt | tan dhārayatīti mahāsamayamantradhṛg bhagavān sarvatathāgatajñānakāyaḥ ||
ratnatrayadharaḥ śreṣṭhas triyānottamadeśaka iti || (MNS 6.24cd) ratnatrayadhara iti | ratnānāṃ trayaṃ ratnatrayaṃ durlabhatvān mahārghatvāt prakāśakatvāc ca ratnānīva ratnāni trīṇi buddhadharmasaṃghākhyāni tāni dhārayatīti ratnatrayadharaḥ | sarveṣām abhigamanīyānāṃ yaḥ prathamataram abhigamanam arhati sa śreṣṭha ucyate |
sa ca bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ || triyānottamadeśaka iti | trayāṇāṃ śrāvakapratyekabuddhamahāyānānām uttamadeśaka iti sarvasattvānāṃ gotrendriyāśayānuśayajñatvāt ||
amoghapāśo vijayī vajrapāśo mahāgrahaḥ | vajrāṅkuśo mahāpāśa iti || (MNS 6.25abc) tatra amoghapāśa iti | amogho 'bandhyaḥ pāśa iva pāśo yasyāsāv amoghapāśaḥ | bodhicittavajravineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ tathāpratibhāsanāt || vijayīti trailokyasamāpatri(?)samādhimukhyena (!) || vajrapāśa iti ratnasambhavadvāreṇa || mahāgraha iti |
grahaṇaṃ graho mahāṃś cāsau grahaś ca mahāgraho vajranigaḍaḥ sa ca amitābhasvabhāvena || vajrāṅkuśa iti | akṣobhyarūpeṇa | mahāpāśa iti pāśastobha (!?) ity āveśaparyāyaḥ | mahāṃś cāsau pāśaś ca mahāpāśo vajrāveśaḥ sa cāmoghasiddhirūpeṇeti |
tato 'sau bhagavān ity ebhir mahāvairocanādibhir abhidhāyakaiḥ śabdasamūhair nāmamantrākṣarapadaiḥ prathamāntavibhaktyutpannair abhidheyo veditavya iti ||
tataś cāsminn api pūrvavat mahāvairocanādayaḥ śuklavarṇāś cakrakhaḍgapāṇayo bhūtvā viniṣkrāntā viniṣkramya ca sarvabuddhakṣetreṣu ca gatvā sattvān svavimokṣamukhena saṃśodhya punar āgamya nāmamantrākṣarāṇi bhūtvā svacakre cakrībhūya sthitāni paśyet |
tataḥ pūrvavan mantramukhacaryācārī bodhisattvo nāmasaṅgītiṃ paṭhaṃs tān eva guṇagaṇasamūhān avāpnotīti || dvitīyasyaiva mahāvairocanacakrasya nāmamantrākṣarapadān śāśvatādikramabhedena ṣaḍviṃśat ṣaḍviṃśat pravibhajya pūrvavat sarvam anuvidheyam ity āmnāyaḥ ||
gāthāś ca pādonapañcaviṃśatiḥ | nāmamantrākṣaṇy ekāśītiśatam aṅkenāpi || 181 ||
āryanāmasaṅgītiṭīkāyāṃ nāmamantrārthāvalokinyāṃ suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānastutyadhikāraḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ||   ||
ataḥ paraṃ tṛtīyam ādarśajñānasvabhāvaṃ krodhacakraṃ trailokyavijayarūpeṇa bhagavataḥ sarvatathāgatajñānastutim āha || vajrabhairavabhīkara iti | (MNS 6.25d = ~7.1d) dvandvena nirdeśaḥ | tataś cāyam artho bhavati | vajrabhairavaś ca bhīkaraś ca vajrabhairavabhīkaraḥ | tatra vajrabhairavatantre dvāv imau paripaṭhitatvāt ||
yaduta vajrabhairavo vajrabhīkaraś ca bhāveva svabhāvo yasya sa vajrabhairavabhīkaro bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ || kasmād ity ata āha || dveṣavineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ saumyatānupayogāt || tathā coktaṃ śrīsamvaratantre | atyantaduṣṭaraudreṣu saumyatā nopayujyate | prajñopāyamayaṃ manyuṃ cakruḥ sarvatathāgatā iti ||
krodharāṭ ṣaṇmukho bhīmaḥ ṣaṇnetraḥ ṣaḍbhujo balīti || (MNS 7.1ab) tatra krodharāḍ iti śrīkrodhavije paṭhyate | krodhena rājyata iti krodharāṭ || tathā coktaṃ || athaiṣa mañjughoṣasya nāmno krodho yamāntakaḥ | dṛṣṭvā tam itare krodhāḥ sarve vigalitāyudhā iti | ata eva krodharāḍ iti ||
ṣaṇmukha iti śrīmāyājālatantre || ṣaṇ mukhāni yasyāsau ṣaṇmukhaḥ karacaraṇayor apy arthāt ṣaṭtvaṃ veditavyaṃ | sa ca bhagavān bhīma iti Vajrapāṇyabhiṣekatantre bhīmo bhayānakaḥ ||
tathā ca sarvāṅgeṣu sarvaromakūpeṣu vajratejaḥprabhṛtīni maṇḍalāni niścārya teṣu maṇḍaleṣu krodharājān nānāveśān nānāpraharaṇopetān bhrūbhaṅgakuñcitalalāṭān mahāśmaśrukarālanayanavadanān saṃsphārya bhayopadarśanād bhīma ity ucyate ||
ṣaṇnetra iti | tatraiva ṣaṇ netrāṇi yasya sa ṣaṇnetraḥ | atraikadeśena stutiḥ | avayavasamudāyopacārāt | yathā bhīmaseno bhīmaḥ | satyabhāmā bhāmeti | ṣaḍbhuja iti śrīvajramaṇḍālaṅkāratantre | ṣaḍ bhujā yasyāsau ṣaḍbhujaḥ || balīti | balaṃ kāyikaṃ sāmarthyaṃ tad asyāstīti balī | śrīMahāvidyottamatantra mahābala iti prasiddhaḥ ||
daṃṣṭrākarālakaṅkālo halāhalaśatānana iti || (MNS 7.1cd) tatra daṃṣṭrākarāla iti | śrīVajraghanoccaye | daṃṣṭre karālajāgre bhayānake yasyāsau daṃṣṭrākarālaḥ || kaṅkāla iti śrīCakrasamvare || halāhalaśatānana iti | ayam api pūrvavad dvandvanirdeśaḥ | halāhala iti śrīAmoghapāśatantre | śatānana iti śrīVajraśekharatantre |
śatāny ānanāni mukhāni yasya sa śatānanaḥ ||
yamāntako vighnarāṭ (!) vajravego bhayaṅkara iti || (MNS 7.2ab) tatra yamāntaka iti śrīSamāje | yamasyāntako vināśako yamāntakaḥ || vighnasarāḍ (!) iti rājata iti rāṭ | vighnānāṃ gaṇapatiprabhṛtīnāṃ rāḍ vighnarāṭ | amṛtakuṇḍaliḥ | sa Māyājālādau pratīta iti || vajravega iti | śrīMahāsamayatantre |
vajrasya vego vajravegas tadvad vego yasya sa vajravegaḥ || bhayaṅkara iti | śrīGuhyendutilake bhayaṃ karotīti bhayaṅkaraḥ sarvaduṣṭasaṃtrāsanāt ||
vighuṣṭavajro hṛdvajro māyāvajro mahodara iti || (MNS 7.2cd) tatra vighuṣṭavajra iti | śrīAdvayasamatāvijaye | viśeṣair ghuṣṭo vighuṣṭaḥ | mahāśabda ity arthaḥ | sa eva vajro abhedyatvād yasya sa vighuṣṭavajraḥ || hṛdvajra iti | śrīGuhyendutilaka eva | hṛdi vajraṃ yasya sa hṛdvajraḥ || māyāvajra iti | asminn eva ṣoḍaśasāhasrike paṭhyate ||
māyā nirmāṇaṃ tad eva vajram abhedyarūpatvād yasya sa māyāvajraḥ || mahodara iti | tatraiva | mahad udaraṃ yasyāsau mahodaraḥ ||
kuliśeśo vajrayonir vajramaṇḍo nabhopama iti | (MNS 7.3ab) tatra kuliśeśa iti | kuliśo vajraṃ tasyeśaḥ prabhur ity arthaḥ | sa ca śrīVajrapātālatantre || vajrayonir iti | śrīBhūtaḍāmaratantre | vajraṃ yoniḥ kāraṇaṃ yasya sa vajrayoniḥ || vajramaṇḍa iti | śrīVajramaṇḍālaṃkāre | vajraṃ maṇḍo yasya sa vajramaṇḍaḥ sāra ity arthaḥ || nabhopama iti |
śrīGuhyakośe | na bhavatīti nabha ākāśaṃ | utpattimatām avakāśadānāt svayaṃ cāsaṃskṛtatvāt | na bhavatīty arthaḥ | tac ca śūnyatāsvarūpaṃ tenopamā yasya sa tathoktaḥ | śūnyatopama ity arthaḥ ||
acalaikajaṭāṭopo gajacarmapaṭārdradhṛg iti || (MNS 7.3cd) tatra acalaikajaṭāṭopa iti dvandvanirdeśaḥ | tataś cāyam arthaḥ | acalaś caikajaṭāṭopaś ca acalaikajaṭāṭopaḥ | tatrācalaḥ śrīMāyājālādau ekajaṭāṭopaḥ śrīVajrakirīṭitantre | na cālyate na kalpyate sarvamārair ity acalaḥ |
ekā cāsau jaṭā ca ekajaṭā tayā āṭopaḥ sauṣṭhavaṃ yasyāsāv ekajaṭāṭopaḥ || gajacarmapaṭārdradhṛg iti | śrīCakrasamvare | gajasya carma gajacarma paṭaś cāsāv ārdraś ca paṭārdraḥ | gajacarmaiva paṭārdro gajacarmapaṭārdraḥ | taṃ dhārayatīti gajacarmapaṭārdradhṛk ||
hāhākāro mahāghoro hīhīkāro bhayānaka iti || (MNS 7.4ab) tatra hāhākāra iti | śrīKrodhavijaye hāhābhūtaṃ nāma maṇḍalaṃ | tatra hāhā kṛtvā tiṣṭhatīti hāhākāra ity ucyate || mahāghora iti | tatraiva | mahāṃś cāsau ghoraś ca mahāghora iti | hīhīkāra iti śrīKrodhendutilake | tadanukaraṇenotthānāt || bhayānaka iti | śrīSarvatantrasamuccaye ||
aṭṭahāso mahāhāso vajrahāso mahārava iti || (MNS 7.4cd) [*] tatrāṭṭahāsa iti | śrīAsādhāraṇaguhyālaṅkāravyūhatantre | hasanaṃ hāsaḥ | aṭṭair uccair hāso [*] 'ṭṭahāsaḥ || mahāhāsa iti | nairuttaryeṇa || vajrahāsa iti | [*] jñānahāsaḥ sa ca mānasaḥ || mahārava iti | trailokye pūrakatvena | mahāṃś cāsau ravaś ca mahāravaḥ |
ete catvāro 'pi tatraiva paṭhyante ||
vajrasattvo [*] mahāsattvo vajrarājo mahāsukha iti || (MNS 7.5ab) tatra vajrasattva iti | Paramādye | mahāsattva iti mahāvajradharaḥ śrīSamāje || vajrarāja iti Guhyakośe | mahāsukha iti śrīSamvare || tatra mahāsukha iti yat tāthāgatam anāsravaṃ sukhaṃ tan mahāsukha ity ucyate | tatraivoktaṃ sukhaṃ śam iti vikhyātaṃ sarvabauddhaṃ mahāsukham iti ||
vajracaṇḍo mahāmodo vajrahūṃkārahūṃkṛtir iti || (MNS 7.5cd) tatra vajracaṇḍa iti | śrīVajraguhyālaṅkāratantre | vajrasya caṇḍo vajracaṇḍaś caṇḍo vega ucyate śīghragamanatvāt || mahāmoda iti | śrīGuhyamaṇitilake mahāṃś cāsau modaś ca mahāmodaḥ || vajrahūṃkārahūṃkṛtir iti | śrīTattvasaṅgrahe |
vajrahūṃkārakārasya (!) hūṃkṛtayaś catvāro hūṃkārās te vidyante yasyāsau vajrahūṃkārahūṃkṛtiḥ ||

athedānīṃ tṛtīyasya krodhacakrasyotsargavidhānam āha || sarvatathāgatajñānasattvahṛdayākṣarān sa eva bhagavān mañjuśrīr dveṣacaritasattvānugrahārthaṃ yogacaryākriyātantroktakrodharūpāṇy ālambya trailokyadamanāya tataś ca svakāyāt samantajvālāgarbhāḥ sabhṛkuṭībhrūbhaṅgākuñcitalalāṭavikaṭadaṃṣṭrākarālamukhāḥ |

ata eva mañjuśriyā sarvatathāgatajñānasattvena skandhādīnām ātmāditvenānupalambha eva paraḥ pramāṇatvenopadiṣṭaḥ | bhūteti bhūtyasatyaṃ tathatāyā avikalpanāmātratvāt | na caivam āśaṅkanīyam ātmādyabhāve ko dharmāṇāṃ svabhāvaḥ kiṃ vā phalam iti |

anirgamasthānīyatvāt | tasya vidāraṇaḥ | bhūtārthabhāvanāprakarṣaparyantagamanāt ||
śamitāśeṣasaṃkleśaḥ saṃsārārṇavapāraga iti || (MNS 8.8ab) tatra śamitāśeṣasaṃkleśa iti || tridhā saṃkleśā iti | kleśasaṃkleśaḥ karmasaṃkleśaḥ janmasaṃkleśaś ca | tatra kleśasaṃkleśaḥ avidyātṛṣṇopādānāni | karmasaṃkleśaḥ saṃskāro bhavaś ca | janmasaṃkleśaḥ śeṣāṇy aṅgāni || punar api dvividhā saṃkleśaḥ | hetusaṃkleśaḥ phalasaṃkleśaś ca |
tatra hetusaṃkleśaḥ kleśakarmasvabhāvair aṅgaiḥ | phalasaṃkleśaś ca śeṣaiḥ | bhūyo 'pi sapta saṃkleśāḥ saptavidho hetuḥ | viparyāsahetur avidyā | ākṣepahetuḥ saṃskāraḥ | upanayanahetur vijñānaṃ | parigrahahetur nāmarūpaṣaḍāyatane | upabhogahetuḥ sparśavedane | ākarṣaṇahetus tṛṣṇopādānabhavāḥ | udvegahetuś ca jātijarāmaraṇe |
sarvaś caiṣa saṃkleśo 'bhūtaparikalpāt pravarttate | sarvaśamita upaśamite | aśeṣo niḥśeṣaḥ saṃkleśo yasyāsau tathā | yata evaṃ tasmāt saṃsārārṇavapāraga ity ucyate ||
jñānābhiṣekamakuṭaḥ samyaksaṃbuddhabhūṣaṇa iti || (MNS 8.8cd) tatra jñānābhiṣekamakuṭa iti | abhiṣekārthaṃ makuṭaḥ | abhiṣekamakuṭaḥ samantabhadrajñānābhiṣekamakuṭaḥ sama (!?) yasyāsau jñānābhiṣekamakuṭaḥ | tataś ca samyaksambuddhabhūṣaṇa iti | tair alaṃkṛtatvāt ||
triduḥkhaduḥkhaśamanas tryanto 'nantas trimuktiga iti || (MNS 8.9ab) tatra triduḥkhaduḥkhaśamana iti | dvandvanirdeśaḥ | triduḥkheti | duḥkhaduḥkhatā upādānaduḥkhatā lakṣaṇaduḥkhatā ca | duḥkhasatyavedanānimittārthena yatkiṃcid veditam idam atra duḥkhasyeti kṛtvā vedanānimittaṃ punar vedanāsthānīyā dharmā veditavyāḥ || tathā coktaṃ ||
duḥkhaṃ saṃsāriṇaḥ skandhā iti | duḥkheti tannimittaprapattitaḥ samudayasatyaṃ duḥkhasa[66 not! see below; actually: 68]tyanimittāyā pratipattiḥ [|] śamana iti tayoḥ śamanārthena nirodhasatyaṃ | ata āha | triduḥkhaduḥkhaśamano bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ || tryanta iti | trayāṇāṃ satyānām anto mārgasatyaṃ ||
tataś caturaśītidharmaskandhasahasrāṇi mārgasatyam iti vacanāt | tatra sattvānāṃ caturaśīticittacaritasahasrapratipakṣodayena caturaśītidharmaskandhopadeśāt || ata evāha || ananta iti || trimuktiga iti | tisro muktayaḥ śrāvakādibhedena tāṃ muktiṃ gacchatīti trimuktigo bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ ||
sarvāvaraṇanirmukta ākāśasamatāṅ gata iti || (MNS 8.9cd) tatra sarvāvaraṇanirmukta iti | sarvāṇi ca āvaraṇāni ceti sarvāvaraṇāni kleśakarmavipākajñeyasamāpatyāvaraṇabhedāt | pañca sarvāvaraṇānīty ucyante | tair nirmukto bhagavān sarvatathāgatajñānakāyaḥ || ata evāha || ākāśasamatāṅ gata iti sarvāvaraṇanirmuktatvāt ||
sarvakleśamalātītas tryadhvānadhvagatiṃ gata iti || (MNS 8.10ab) tatra sarvakleśamalātīta iti | sarvakleśā uktalakṣaṇās ta eva malās tān atīto nikrāntaḥ sarvakleśopakleśamalāpagatatvāt | stryadhveti (!) | atītānāgatavarttamānasvarūpatvāt tryadhva iti triḥkālarūpatayā || anadhva[66 !!!]gatiṅ gata iti | nādhvā anadhvā |
tataś cātītasyātītatvāt anāgatasyāsaṃprāptatvāt varttamānasya cobhayabhāvāt sthitir nāsti ataś ca trailokyāsiddheḥ tasyānadhvano gatir anadhvagatiḥ | tāṃ tryadhvānadhvagatiṃgataḥ ||
sarvasattvamahānāgo guṇaśekharaśekhara iti || (MNS 8.10cd) tatra sarvasattvamahānāga iti | viśiṣṭādhigamadharmalābhān mahānāgaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ mahānāgaḥ | sarvasattvamahānāgaḥ pradhāna ity arthaḥ | ata eva guṇaśekharaśekhara iti |
sakalalaukikalokottarāṇām ābhigāmikānāṃ guṇā<guṇaśekharāṇām api śekharaḥ>nāṃ śekharaś cūḍāmaṇir guṇaśekhara ity ucyate | yathā puṣpāṇāṃ mūrdhni vyavasthitānāṃ loke śekhara iti vyapadeśaḥ | tathā sarvaguṇagaṇānām upary avasthānāt guṇaśekharaśekhara iti ||
sarvopadhivinirmukto vyomavartmani susthita iti || (MNS 8.11ab) tatra sarvopadhivinirmukta iti || upadhayaś catvāraḥ | dharmārthakāmanītilakṣaṇāḥ | taiś caturbhir api dharmair vimuktatvāt sarvopadhivinirmukta iti | sarvadharmair akampyatvāt nirvikāratvāc ca || vyomavartmani susthita iti ākāśamārge prajñāpāramitāyāṃ supratiṣṭhitatvāt ||
tathā coktaṃ Āryāṣṭasāhasryāṃ ākāśabhāvanaiṣā subhūte yaduta prajñāpāramitābhāvaneti ||
mahācintāmaṇidharaḥ sarvaratnottamo vi[67 not!; but 69]bhur iti || (MNS 8.11cd) mahāṃś cāsau cintāmaṇiś ca mahācintāmaṇiḥ | yathābhilaṣitārthasiddhisaṃpādakatvāt tad dhārayatīti mahācintāmaṇidharaḥ | ata eva sarvaratnottama iti | sarvaratnānām uttamatvāt | vibhur iti gatārthaṃ ||
mahākalpataruḥ sphīto mahābhadraghaṭottama iti || (MNS 8.12ab) tatra mahākalpatarur iti | mahāṃś cāsau kalpataruś ca mahākalpataruḥ | abhimatārthasiddhidāyakatvāt ata eva sphītaḥ sarvasaṃmṛddhatvāt || mahābhadraghaṭottama iti | bhadraghaṭaś cāsāv uttamaś ca bhadraghaṭottamaḥ mahāṃś cāsau bhadraghaṭottamaś ca mahābhadraghaṭottamaḥ ||

śubhāśubhajñaḥ kālajñaḥ samayajñaḥ samayī vibhur iti || (MNS 8.13ab) [67 !!!] tatra śubhāśubhajña iti | śubhaṃ cāśubhaṃ ca śubhāśubhaṃ | kuśalākuśalam ity arthaḥ | taj jānātīti śubhāśubhajño bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ | kvacid aśubham api vetti sattvārthavaśāt || tathā coktaṃ || sadayasyāśubhaṃ nāstīti || kālajña iti | kālaṃ jānātīti kālajñaḥ |
ayam avatārakālo 'yaṃ dharmadeśanākālo 'yaṃ sattvārthakriyākālo 'yaṃ upekṣākālo 'yaṃ parinirvāṇakāla ity evambhāgīyaḥ || samayajña iti | hṛnmudrāmantravidyānāṃ svarūpaṃ jānāti vettīti samayajñaḥ || ata eva samayī || vibhur iti gatārthaṃ ||
sattvendriyajño velajño vimuktitrayakovida iti || (MNS 8.13cd) tatra sattvendriyajña iti | sattvānām indriyāṇi sattvendriyāṇi sattvasambandhīni mṛdvindriyādīni tāni jānātīti sattvendriyajñaḥ | velajña iti | velā jānātīti velajñaḥ | yācakasattvānām abhiprāyaṃ viditvaiva kāle dīyamānasya dānāder akṣayatvāt || vimuktitrayakovida iti |

sarvamāṅgalyamāṅgalyaḥ kīrtir lakṣmīr yaśaḥ śubha iti || (MNS 8.14cd) tatra sarvamāṅgalyamāṅgalya iti | maṅgale sādhu māṅgalya iti sarvamāṅgalyānāṃ sarvapavitratarāṇāṃ madhye māṅgalyaḥ || kīrtir iti | viśvantaraśaśajātakādāv anekādbhutasattvārthakaraṇāt | adyāpy avinasvarī kīrtir iti tanmūrtatvāt tasya bhagavataḥ || lakṣmīr iti |
ekāgracittatayā bhāvanābhiyuktānāṃ kulaputrādīnāṃ sarvasampadyodhārabhūtatvāt (!) lakṣmīr bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ || yaśa iti dānādīnāṃ prakarṣaparyantagamanāt | sarvadoṣamalābhāvāc ca nirmalayaśaḥpuñjatvāt yaśa ucyate | śubha iti | daśakuśalakarmapathātmakatvāt ||
mahotsavo mahāśvāso mahānando mahāratir iti || (MNS 8.15ab) tatra mahotsava iti | mahāṃś cāsāv utsavaś ca mahotsavaḥ | yāyāvara ucyate | anusamvatsaraṃ śrāddhaṃ yaḥ karoti yathāvidhi [|] nityapravṛttayāgaś ca sa yāyāvara ucyate | mokṣamārgagataṃ nityam anye yāyāvaraṃ vidur iti |
ubhayathāpi bhagavān mañjuśrīr eva yāyāvaraḥ satatapravṛttadānatvāt nityaṃ ca mokṣamārgagatatvāt kuśalapakṣaikanimnatayā | mahotsavatvāt || mahāśvāsa iti | āśvāsanam āśvāsaḥ mahāṃś cāsāv āśvāsaś ca mahāśvāsaḥ śrīvajrasattvādisamādhinā rāgādyadhimuktikānāṃ sattvānāṃ samāśvāsanāt sa tathā || mahānanda iti | mahāṃś cāsāv ānandaś ca mahānandaḥ |

satkāraḥ satkṛtir bhūtiḥ pramodaḥ śrīyaśaspatir iti || (MNS 8.15cd) tatra satkāra iti | traidhātukeṣu sanmānapūjārhatvāt | satkṛtir iti | karaṇaṃ kṛtiḥ satāṃ kṛtiḥ santo hi śrāvakādayas teṣāṃ yathārthaṃ pratipattiḥ | tathā coktaṃ | satkṛtyādhyāpayati yaḥ sāṅgaṃ vedaṃ yathāvidhi | vivṛṇoti ca mantrārthaṃ satkṛtiḥ so 'bhidhīyate ||
bhagavān mañjuśrīś ca satkṛtya paramārthavedasya dvādaśāṅgasya pravacanasyādhyāpanāt sarvamantrāṇāṃ ca yathāvad vivaraṇāt sa[69 not!; rather 66:]tkṛtir ity ucyate || bhūtir iti | sarvavibhūtyādhārarūpatvāt || pramoda iti sarvapraharṣasvabhāvatvāt || śrīyaśaspatir iti śriyo devatyas tāsāṃ yaśāṃsi sukhasaumanasyāni teṣāṃ patiḥ svāmī bhagavān ||
vareṇyo varadaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ śaraṇyaḥ śaraṇottama iti || (MNS 8.16ab) tatra vareṇya iti | sarvābhigamanīyānām agratvāt || varada iti | varaṃ dadātīti varadaḥ | yathābhiprāyaparipūraṇāt || śreṣṭha iti | bāhyaśrāvakādīnām uttamatvāt || śaraṇya iti | dīnānāṃ duḥkhitānām aśaraṇānāṃ śaraṇārhatvāt || ata eva śaraṇottama iti |
mahābhayāriḥ pravaro niḥśeṣabhayanāśana iti || (MNS 9.16cd) tatra mahābhayārir iti | daivāny aṣṭau mahābhayāni vyādhir maraṇaṃ durbhikṣāgnir udakaṃ rakṣaḥ śalabhā mūṣikāś ca teṣām ariḥ | vyādhyādinighnatinatayā (-nirghātanatayā!?) ity upadravanāśakatvāt || ata eva pravara ity ucyate || niḥśeṣabhayanāśana iti |
niravaśeṣabhayāny aṣṭau mahābhayāni tāni harikariśikhītyādi | teṣāṃ niḥśeṣāṇāṃ bhayānāṃ nāśakatvāt | niḥśeṣabhayanāśano bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ | tadanusmaraṇamātreṇāśeṣabhayanāśanād iti ||
idānīṃ sarvasattvānāṃ jagadvinayasvabhāvena tattadbhaktānāṃ tattadrūpeṇāpi bhāsata iti || [66 not!; rather 69] śikhī śikhaṇḍī jaṭilo jaṭī mauṇḍī kirīṭimān iti || (MNS 9.17ab) tatra śikhīti | śikhāsyāstīti śikhī | kvacid bālarūpeṇa pratibhāsata iti || śikhaṇḍīti | kārttikeyarūpeṇa | jaṭila iti | jaṭā vidyate yasyāsau jaṭilaḥ | jaṭādhararūpeṇa |
jaṭīti | ekaśikhārūpeṇa || mauṇḍīti muṇḍadhararūpeṇa || kirīṭimān iti | kirīṭir mukuṭaḥ sa vidyate yasyāsau kirīṭimān ādityādirūpeṇa | yadyadvineyānāṃ sattvānāṃ tattadrūpeṇa eteṣāṃ cittasantāneṣu pratibhāsodayāt || tathā coktaṃ ||
pañcānanaḥ pañcaśikhaḥ pañcacīrakaśekhara iti || (MNS 8.17cd) ayam anuṣṭubardhaś cādibuddhasya gurūpadeśenādiyoge draṣṭavyas tatraiva vyākhyātatvāc ca punar iha nocyate ||
mahāvratadharo mauñjī brahmacārī vratottama iti || (MNS 8.18ab) tatra mahāvratadhara iti | kvacit pañcamudrālaṅkārarūpeṇāpi pratibhāsanāt || mauñjīti muñjamekhalāvratadhāraṇarūpeṇa || brahmacārīti anantakalpabrahmacaryācaraṇāt || vikārahetāv api yasya ceto na vikriyata ity asāv eva bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ | brahmacārīrūpeṇa kvacit pratibhāsate ||
vratottama iti alaṃ paryāptaṃ prativedaṃ dvādaśa varṣāṇi brahmacaryaṃ caritvā gṛhāśramam akṛtvaiva bhikṣutvena vānamprasthyabhāvena vā yo janmasakalaṃ nītavān tam alam uṣitabrahmacaryam āhur vratottamaṃ ca | ata eva vratānāṃ sarveṣām uttamatvāt mañjuśrīr eva yujyate | aparimitakalpajñānacaryācaraṇāt ||
mahātapās taponiṣṭhaḥ snātako gautamo 'graṇīr iti || (MNS 8.18cd) tatra mahātapā iti | mahat tapo yasyāsau mahātapās tilārdhaṃ bhakṣayitvā ṣaṭ varṣāṇi duṣkaracaryāyām avasthānāt | tatpradarśakatvaṃ tu tadvineyānām āvarṣanārthaṃ (=āvarjanārtham!) | tataś cāsau bhagavān mañjuśrīr mahātapā ity ucyate || taponiṣṭha iti |
sarvasya lokasya yat tapa indriyopaśamalakṣaṇaṃ tasya niṣṭhā avasānaṃ yatra sa taponiṣṭhaḥ sa ca mañjuśrīḥ || snātaka iti | adhītavedaḥ samāptavrataḥ snātakaḥ | sarvavratānāṃ sarvavidyānāṃ ca paryantagamanād āryamañjuśrīr eva snātaka ity ucyate || gautama iti gotranāma tenāsau bhagavān vyapadiśyate | ata evāgraṇīr iti |
svagotreṣu jyeṣṭhatvaṃ sarvajñajñānalābhāt ||
brahmavid brāhmaṇo brahmā brahmanirvāṇam āptavān iti || (MNS 8.19ab) tatra brahmavid iti | brahmaśabdena vedo ucyate | tān jānāti vettīti brahmavit anūcāna ucyate | Brahmādayaḥ kilānūcānāḥ | evaṃ hi smaryate | Viṣṇur Maheśvaraś ca prathamataraṃ vedam ākāśastham anugamya proktavanto Prajāpataye | Prajāpatir Indrāya | Indro Vasiṣṭhāya |
Vasiṣṭhaḥ Pārāśarāya (!) | Pārāśaraḥ Kṛṣṇadvaipāyanāya | Kṛṣṇadvaipāyano Jaimināya (!) | ityādi | sa evaṃlakṣaṇo bhagavān mañjuśrīr evānūcāno yuktaḥ | yato bhagavān yānatrayasaṃgṛhītaṃ niḥśeṣaṃ pravacanam anugamya vineyebhya uktavān | tasmād asau mañjuśrīr brahmavidrūpeṇāpi tadbhaktānāṃ pratibhāsata iti || brāhmaṇa iti |
tapaḥ śrutaṃ ca yoniś ca etad brāhmaṇakāraṇam iti | yato bhagavān evānantakālajanitena tapasānantāparyantena śrutena yuktatvāt | anāsravamārgotpattiyonitvāc ca guṇatrayeṇa yukta iti | paro brāhmaṇo bhagavān || atha vā mantrabrāhmaṇabhedena dvidhā vedaḥ | tatra yo 'sau brāhmaṇātmako vedaḥ sa bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ |
tatsādhyasya kāryasya bhagavataiva sādhyatvāt | tadrūpeṇāpi kvacit pratibhāsate || brahmeti | rūpadhātau prathamadhyānasamāpanno brahmā gṛhyate | tadrūpeṇāpi pratibhāsamānatvāt || brahmanirvāṇam iti | brahmaṇo nirvāṇaṃ brahmanirvāṇaṃ tac cānandaṃ tathā coktam ānandaṃ brahmaṇo rūpaṃ cetaneti tad ucyate | tadrūpeṇāpi bhagavān eva || āptavān iti |
avisaṃvādaka āptaḥ || tathā coktaṃ || pitṛmitrādir āptaḥ syād aduṣṭakāraṇaḥ kvacit | sarvajño 'tīndriyārtheṣu śrutiś ced yady akṛtrimeti | mañjuśrīś ca niratiśayābhyāṃ jñānānugrahopāyābhyāṃ yogāt parama āptaḥ | sa vidyate yasya sa āptavān ||
muktir mokṣo vimokṣāṅgo vimuktiḥ śāntatā śiva iti || (MNS 8.19cd) tatra muktir iti śūnyatādarśanaṃ | tathā coktaṃ Dharmakīrtinā || muktis tu śūnyatādṛṣṭes tadarthāśeṣabhāvaneti || tataś ca bhagavān eva muktiḥ | śūnyatāvinirbhāgavarttitvāt || mokṣa iti |
vairāgyapratipannasya yoginas tasyārthapratisaṃvedino dharmapratisaṃvedinaś cotpadyate prāmodyaṃ pramuditasya prītir jāyate prītamanasaḥ kāyaḥ prasrabhyate | prasrabdhakāyaḥ sukhaṃ vedayate sukhitasya cittaṃ samādhīyate samāhitacitto yathābhūtaṃ prajānāti | yathābhūtaṃ paśyati | yathābhūtaṃ prajānan | yathābhūtaṃ paśyan |
nirvidyate nirviṇṇo virajyate | virakto vimucyata ity evaṃ yathābhūtaṃ paśyato vimuktacittatā vyavasthāpitā bhagavatā | tasya bhūta + + tvāt (= bhūtadarśitvāt) || tathā coktaṃ || asaṃlīnena kāyena vedanām adhivāsayet | pradyotasyeva nirvāṇaṃ vimokṣas tasya cetasa iti || Kambalācāryeṇāpy uktaṃ || rāgādimalinaṃ cittaṃ saṃsāras tadviviktatā |
saṃkṣepāt kathito mokṣaḥ prahīṇāvaraṇair jinair iti || tad evaṃvidhaḥ śrāvakādikānāṃ mokṣas tadyāne bhagavatā vyavasthāpitaḥ sa ca bhagavān mañjuśrīr eva veditavyas tasya sarvagatatvāt ||
vimokṣāṅga iti | vimokṣasyāṅgāni vimokṣāṅgāni | vimokṣo bodhiḥ | tasyāṅgāni sapta bodhyaṅgāni | aṣṭau mārgāṅgāni ca || yaduta smṛtisaṃbodhyaṅgam | dharmapravicayasaṃbodhyaṅgam | prītisambodhyaṅgam | prasrabdhisaṃbodhyaṅgam | samādhisaṃbodhyaṅgam | vīryasaṃbodhyaṅgam | upekṣāsaṃbodhyaṅgam iti ||
etāni ca saṃbodher aṅgāny avayavabhūtāni iti bodhyaṅgāni | etebhyaḥ kleśāvaraṇapratipakṣabhūtebhyo anantaraṃ jñeyāvaraṇaprahāṇāya bhāvanāmārgasaṃprabhāvitāni aṣṭau mārgāṅgāṇi || tadyathā samyagdṛṣṭiḥ | saṃyaksaṃkalpaḥ | samyagvāk | samyakkarmāntaḥ | samyagājīvaḥ | samyagvyāyāmaḥ | samyaksmṛtiḥ | samyaksaṃbodhiś ca |
sa eva darśanamārgo bhāvanāmārgaś ca | vimokṣasya bodher aṅgānīti kṛtvā | sa evāsau bhagavān mañjuśrīr vimokṣāṅga ity ucyate || tayos tatsvabhāvatvāt ||
vimuktir iti rāgādibhyo bandhanebhyaḥ || tathā ca || trīṇi bandhāni (: bandhanāni) | rāgo bandhanaṃ | moho bandhanaṃ | dveṣo bandhanam iti | (: dveṣo bandhanaṃ | moho bandhanam iti)[82 not!; instead: 84 (darkened)] [+ + +] ti kramād (: etair baddhānāṃ traidhātukānatikramād) bandhanāni | tair vimuktatvāt vimuktir ity ucyate ||
tathā cātra vakṣyaty anuśaṃsāyāṃ vimuktiḥ sarvabandhanānām iti || śāntateti | sarvacittopa [+ + +] vikalpānāṃ (: sarvacittopaplavānāṃ grāhakādivikalpānāṃ) śāntatvāt śāntatety ucyate || śiva iti |
nirodhādibhir aṣṭābhir vivādāspadair vimukto yaḥ pratītyasamutpādaḥ sarvaprapañcopaśamalakṣaṇaḥ sa śivaḥ sa ca bhagavān sarvatathāgatajñānasattvaḥ śūnyatājñānasvabhāvatvāt śiva ity ucyate || tathā coktam Āryapādaiḥ (: āryadevapādaiḥ !) | anirodham anutpādam anucchedam aśāśvatam | anekārtham anānārtham anāgamam anirgamam ||
yaḥ pratītyasamutpādaṃ prapañcopaśamaṃ śivam ityādi ||
nirvāṇaṃ nirvṛttiḥ śāntiḥ śreyo niryāṇam antagaḥ | sukhaduḥkhāntakṛn niṣṭhā vairāgyam upadhikṣaya iti || (MNS 8.20) tatra nirvāṇam ity uddeśaḥ śeṣāṇi padāni kulabhedena tasya vyākhyānāni [+ + +] (: nirvāṇasya svarūpam āha) Ratnāvalyām | na cābhāvo 'pi nirvāṇaṃ kuta evāsya bhāvatā | bhāvābhāvaparāmarśakṣayo nirvāṇam ucyata iti || nirvṛtir iti |
nirvāṇaṃ nivṛti[+ + +]tanmātrāvalambanatvāt (: nirvā + + + tiḥ sā ca vojyanā? teṣāṃ tanmātrāvalambanatvāt) || śāntir iti | śamanaṃ śāntiḥ | sā ca śrāvakāṇāṃ || śreṣṭho niryāṇam iti | niścayena yānaṃ niryāṇaṃ śreyo nirvāṇa [+ + +] (: niryāṇaṃ pratyekabuddhānāṃ)
[82 !!!] antaga iti | antaṃ vināśaṃ gacchati yātīti | antago | akṣobhyaḥ [|] dveṣacaritānāṃ svavimokṣamukhena saṃśodhanāt || sukhaduḥkhāntakṛd iti | ratnasambhavaḥ | mātsaryacaritānāṃ sattvānāṃ dānaviśuddhipradānasvabhāvena teṣāṃ sukhaduḥkhāntakaraṇāt ||
niṣṭhety amitābhaḥ [|] rāgacaritānāṃ sattvānāṃ rāgaviśuddhidharmatāprakāśanadvāreṇa pratyavekṣaṇājñānasya niṣṭhāgamanāt || vairāgyam ity amoghasiddhiḥ | kausīdyacaritānāṃ sattvānāṃ svavimokṣarūpeṇa teṣāṃ vairāgyam utpādya kuśalapakṣe cittaṃ niyojya nirvāṇapathaprakāśanāt || upadhikṣaya iti vairocanaḥ | upadhir vāsanā tasyāḥ kṣayo vināśaḥ |
upadhikṣayaḥ | mohacaritānāṃ sattvānāṃ yathābhūtadharmatāprakāśanamukhena suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānod[d]yotanāt || tataś ca dvividho hi mokṣaḥ sopadhiśeṣo nirupadhiśeṣaś ca | tatra bāhyādīnāṃ sopadhiśeṣaḥ | samyaksaṃbuddhānāṃ nirupadhiśesaḥ || ata evoktaṃ || savāsanāś ca te doṣā na santy ekasya tāyina iti ||
tataś cobhayanirvāṇasvabhāvenāpi sa eva bhagavān mañjuśrīr eva gantavya ity ata āha nirvāṇam ityādi |
ajayo 'nupamo 'vyakto nirābhāso nirañjana iti || (MNS 8.21ab) tatrājaya iti | parair na jīyata ity ajayaḥ || anupamam iti | sarvopamārahitatvāt | avyakta iti stryādirūpābhāvāt | nirābhāsa iti | ābhāsarahitatvāt | nirañjana iti | nirlepatvāt | yataḥ sa eva bhagavān paramārthasvabhāvatvāt pañcabhir ebhir viśeṣaṇapadair gṛhyate ||
niṣkalaḥ sarvago vyāpī sūkṣmo bījam anāsrava iti || (MNS 8.21cd) tatra niṣkala iti | kalārahitatvāt | sarvaga iti sarvagatatvāt || vyāpīti sthiracalapadārthasvabhāvatvāt | aṇoḥ sūkṣmād apy aṇīyānū (!?) sūkṣmaḥ || bījam iti | dharmādharmānekṣyo(?)vāsanābījakhacitam ālayavijñānaṃ | anāsrava iti mārgasatyasvabhāvatvāt caturvidhaḥ |
asaṃskṛtasvabhāvatvāt | caturvidhaḥ ākāśaṃ pratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ | apratisaṃkhyānirodhaḥ | tathatā ca | <anāsravā mārgasatyan trividhaṃ cāsya py asaṃskṛtam ity ukteḥ | śūnyādibhedāt 4 anāsrava > sa eva bhagavān tatsvabhāvatvāt ||
arajo virajo vimalo vāntadoṣo nirāmaya iti || (MNS 8.22ab) tatra araja iti rāgādirajopagatatvāt | viraja iti | tathatāviśuddheḥ sthityanyathātvābhāvāt | vimala iti | dharmāṇāṃ śūnyatayā malānām āgantukatvāt | sa eva bhagavān vimala ity ucyate | vāntadoṣa iti | vānto nigīrṇo doṣo rāgādir yena sa vāntadoṣaḥ | ata eva nirāmaya iti |
nirgata āmayo vyādhir yasya sa nirāmaya iti | evaṃguṇaviśiṣṭaś ca sa eva bhagavān ||
suprabuddho viśuddhātmā sarvajñaḥ sarvavit para iti || (MNS 8.22cd) tatra suprabuddha iti | suṣṭhu prakarṣeṇa buddhaḥ suprabuddhaḥ yathāvasthitapadārthāviparītāvabodhanāt || vibuddhātmeti | śrāvakādibhyo viśeṣeṇa buddho vibuddhaḥ | tasyātmā śūnyatāsvabhāvaḥ | sa eva yasyāsau vibuddhātmā | sarvajña iti |
sarvaṃ niravaśeṣaṃ jñeyaṃ jānātīti sarvajñaḥ | sarvavid iti | sarvasattvānāṃ cittacaritam āśayānuśayaṃ ca vettīti sarvavit | ata eva para utkṛṣṭo 'sau bhagavān ||
vijñānadharmatātīto jñānam advayarūpadhṛg iti || (MNS 8.23ab) tatra vijñānadharmatātīta iti | vijñāna[ṃ ]cakṣurādivyatiriktam ālayavijñānam asti | tad eva ca sarvabījakaṃ na cakṣurvijñānādīti kuta etat | āgamād yuktitaś ca | tatra yuktir ane[82 !!!]kamukhena savistarā (('sta))tra pratipāditeti nehaṃ pratanyate |
āgamas tu vivādāspadarahitatvāt alpavaktavyatvāc ca vaktavyaḥ [|] tathā coktam Abhidharmasūtre [–] anādikāliko dhātuḥ sarvadharmasamāśrayaḥ | tasmiṃ sati gatīḥ sarvā nirvāṇādhigamo 'pi ceti || tasya dharmatā vijñānadharmatā śūnyatā tayātīto 'tikrāntas tasyā apy anupalabdheḥ || jñānam iti sarvatra cādhipatyaprabhāvitatvāt |
suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānaṃ || ata eva advayarūpadhṛg iti | audāryagāmbhīryātmakatvāt ||
nirvikalpo nirābhogas tryadhvasaṃbuddhakāyakṛd iti || (MNS 8.23cd) tatra nirvikalpa iti | paramārthavikalpe 'pi avika[lpa]tvāt (: avikalpanāt) || tathā coktaṃ [–] paramārthavikalpe 'pi nāvalīyate paṇḍitaḥ [|] ko hi bhedo vikalpasya śubhe vāpy aśubhe 'pi veti || tataś ca sa eva bhagavān nirvikalpaḥ || nirābhoga iti |
nirabhisaṃskāra ity arthaḥ nirgata ābhogo nirābhogaḥ | tataś cāyam artho bhavati | pūrvasmin sābhisaṃskāravāhiniprajñāpracā + + + + dhānaṃ (: prajñādhāra|yet praṇidhānaṃ) cintāmaṇivad avikalpasyāpi mama yathābhavyaṃ sattvārtho bhaviṣyatīti tat pūrvapraṇidhānaṃ tenābhimatam ākṣiptaṃ satataṃ nityam anābhogaṃ praya[82 not!;
