anādinidhano buddha ādibuddho niranvaya iti || (MNS 8.24ab) tatra anādinidhana iti | ādiś ca nidhanaś ca ādinidhane na vidyate ādinidhane yasyāsāv anādinidhanaḥ utpādanirodhābhāvāt tasya bhagavataḥ | buddha iti | atha buddha ity etat kathaṃ bhagavati vartate | kathaṃ ca na syāt budher jñānārthatvāt | tasmāt ktaḥ |
bhāvakarmaṇoḥ prayogo draṣṭavyaḥ | buddhaṃ devadattenādhītaṃ buddhaṃ śāstraṃ devadatteneti karmaṇi vā buddhaśabda iṣyate | iha hi buddhavān sarvadharmān iti | kartari buddhaḥ | sakalapadārthāvabodhinī buddhir asyeti vā akāro matvārthāyo tiśayārthe (!) | atha vā budher avagamanārthatvāt | avagamanaṃ ca gatiḥ |
gatyarthebhyaś ca kto bhāvakarmakartṛṣu bhavatīti kartary astu sarvadharmān svalakṣaṇa[85]sāmānyalakṣaṇato buddhavān iti buddhaḥ sa bhagavān ||
ādibuddha iti | ādāv eva buddha ādibuddhaḥ sa ca pañcajñānātmakaḥ | katham iti cet || atra āha || balaprabhāvitam ādarśajñānaṃ | tatra balāni pañca śraddhāvīryasmṛtisamādhiprajñopalakṣitāni | taiḥ prabhāvitatvāt | bhūtakoṭyā[ṃ ]tadadhigamotkarṣaṃ | abhe (?) viśāradyalakṣaṇaṃ samatājñānaṃ |
tatra vaiśāradyāni jñānavaiśāradyaṃ prahāṇavaiśāradyaṃ saṃkleśahetūpadeśavaiśāradyaṃ vyavadānahetūpadeśavaiśāradyaṃ ceti | tataś caturbhir vaiśāradyaiḥ samatājñānasya lābhāt ||
anamittena (!) sūkṣmakalpanāviśuddhaṃ ataḥ pratisaṃvitsvabhāvaṃ pratyavekṣaṇājñānaṃ || tatra catasraḥ pratisamvidaḥ | arthadharmaniruktipratibhānayogāt tābhiḥ pratyavekṣaṇājñānalābhāt || dharmadhātunā sarvadharmasāratām adhigamāśayavtvaṃ ca tato maitrīkaruṇākāraṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānaṃ || sarvādhipatyajanitatvāt suviśuddhadharmadhātujñānam iti |

jñānaikacakṣur amalo jñānamūrtis tathāgata iti || (MNS 8.24cd) tatra jñānaikacakṣur iti | jñānam evaikaṃ cakṣur yasya sa jñānaikacakṣuḥ || ata eva amala iti | rāgādimalābhāvāt | jñānamūrtir iti | jñānam eva mūrtir na rūpādi yasyāsau jñānamūrtiḥ sa eva tathāgata iti | gatārthaṃ ca ||
vāgīśvaro mahāvādī vādirāḍ vādipuṅgavaḥ | vadatāṃ varo variṣṭho vādisiṃho 'parājita iti || (MNS 8.25) tatra vāgīśvarādīnām aṣṭānāṃ padānāṃ hetuhetumadbhāvena phalasadbhāvo vyākhyeyaḥ || tataś cāha | vāgīśvara iti | vā<cā>gīśvaro (!) vāgīśvaraḥ | ata eva vadatāṃ varo bhavati | mahāvādīti mahāṃś cāsau vādī ca mahāvādī | ata eva variṣṭhaḥ ||

samantadarśī prāmodyas tejomālī sudarśana iti || (MNS 8.26ab) tatra samantadarśīti | samantād draṣṭuṃ śīlaṃ yasyāsau samantadarśī | samantabhadrajñānalābhāt | ata eva bhagavān samantaṃ spharaṇaṃ tviṭ | prāmodya iti | arthadharmapratisaṃvijñāt | tejomālīti | tejasāṃ raśmīnāṃ mālā paṅktiḥ | tejomālāsya vidyate yasya sa tejomālī | sudarśana iti |

satyādiṣu yā vimatis tad eva ripuḥ kuśalapakṣapratibandhakatvāt tasya darpo 'haṃkāras taṃ hantīti | ajñānaripudarpahā || [+ + +] bījaṃ sa eva śṛṅgāradhara iti | śṛṅgaṃ śukraṃ ā samantāt rāti dadhāti iti śṛṅgāra<dhara>ḥ sarvendriyavyāpakatvāt | tad dhārayatīti [+ + +] gatārtaṃ | vīrabībhatsarūpadhṛg iti |
vi[¯]rarasopetā bībhatsarasopetāś ca ye krodhaviśeṣās teṣāṃ rūpaṃ vīrabībhatsarūpaṃ tad dhārayatīti [+ + +] dhṛk ||
bāhudaṇḍaśatākṣepapadanikṣepanartanaḥ | śrīmacchatabhujābhogo gaganābhoganartana iti || (MNS 9.3) tatra bāhudaṇḍaśatākṣepapada[+ + +]daṇḍaśatānām ākṣepo bahirāvartanaṃ padasthānam ālīḍhādi tasya nikṣepaḥ pañcavitastyādi | ābhyāṃ bāhudaṇḍaśatākṣepapada[+ + +]ā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
bhūmicaryānṛtyaṃ darśayitvā bhūyo + + śacaryānṛtyaṃ darśayitum āha || śrīmacchatabhujābhogo gaganābhoga[+ + +] narteśvaraḥ | tasya śata bhujāḥ śrīmacchatabhujāḥ teṣām ābhogaḥ | + + + śrīmacchatabhujāṃ bhogaḥ | tena gagane | ākāśe ābhogaḥ parivartanaṃ [84] bhuṃjaśatānāṃ (!) yasyāsau sa tathoktaḥ ||
ekapādatalākrāntamahīmaṇḍatale sthitaḥ | brahmāṇḍaśikharākrāntapādāṅguṣṭhanakhe sthita iti || (MNS 9.4) tatra ekapādatalākrāntamahīmaṇḍatale sthita iti | ekaś cāsau pādaś ca ekapādaḥ | tasya talaṃ ekapādatalaṃ | mahyā maṇḍatalaṃ mahīmaṇḍatalaṃ lakāralopaṃ kṛtvāyaṃ nirdeśaḥ saddharmapāṭhād vā |
ekapādatalenākrāntaṃ mahīmaṇḍatala ekapādatalākrāntamahīmaṇḍatalaṃ tatra sthita yasya sa ekapādatalākrāntamahīmaṇḍatale sthitaḥ | dvitīyasya vyāpāram āha | brahmāṇḍetyādi | pādasyāṅguṣṭhaḥ pādāṅguṣṭhas tasya nakhaḥ pādāṅguṣṭhanakhaḥ | brahmāṇḍasya śikharaṃ brahmāṇḍaśikharaṃ |
tenākrāntaṃ pādāṅguṣṭhanakhasthitaṃ yasya sa brahmāṇḍaśikharākrāntapādāṅguṣṭhanakhasthitaḥ | anena padadvayena ṛddhiḥ pradarśitā | tasya bhagavataḥ | tadvineyajanāvarjanārthaṃ |
ekārtho 'dvayadharmārthaḥ parārtho 'vinaśvaraḥ | nānāvijñaptirūpārthaś cittavijñānasantatir iti || (MNS 9.5) tatra ekārtha iti || paramārthasvabhāvatvāt || ata evādvayadharmārtha iti | grāhyagrāhakānupapatteḥ || paramārtha iti | arthaprāptipratipattibhedāt tridhaiva paramārthaḥ || avinaśvara iti ākāśatulyatvāt ||
nānāvijñaptirūpārthacittavijñānasantatir iti || nānāvijñaptirūpārtha iti | ṣaṭpravṛttivijñānāni | cittam ity ālayavijñānaṃ | vijñānam iti | kliṣṭaṃ manaḥ | tataś cāyaṃ kramo bhavati | cittād ālayavijñānalakṣaṇāt kliṣṭaṃ manaḥ | tasmāc ca ṣaṭpravṛttivijñānāni viṣayavijñaptilakṣaṇāni |
tadvāsanāś ca punar ālayavijñāne vāsitāc cālayavijñānāt punaḥ kliṣṭaṃ manaḥ tasmāc ca punaḥ pravṛttivijñānānīty evaṃ sūkṣmapratītyasamutpādacakraṃ paribhramati || tathā coktaṃ || vipāko mananākhyaś ca vijñaptir viṣayasya ceti || ataś ca kim uktaṃ bhavati | yāni ṣaḍviṣayavijñaptilakṣaṇāni |
yac ca cittaṃ yac ca kliṣṭaṃ manaḥ tat sarvaṃ sa eva bhagavān ||
aśeṣabhāvārtharatiḥ śūnyatāratir agradhīr iti || (MNS 9.6ab) tatra aśeṣabhāvārtharatir iti | aśeṣā niravaśeṣā ye bhāvārthās teṣu sthityādiṣu ratiḥ prītir lokavyavahāravaśāt || tathā coktaṃ Nāgārjunapādaiḥ || sthitim utpādaṃ bhaṅgaṃ sad asad dhīnaṃ samaṃ viśiṣṭaṃ vā | lokavyavahāravaśād vadanti buddhā na tattvavaśād iti || śūnyatāratir iti |
śūnyatāyāṃ ratiḥ prītiḥ śūnyatāratiḥ | sarvadharmatathatāsvabhāvatvāt paramārthatas tu tasyā apy anupalabdheḥ || ata evāgradhīr iti ||
bhavarāgādyatītaś ca bhavatrayamahāratir iti || (MNS 9.6cd) bhavarāgādyatīta iti | tatra kāmabha¯vo rūpabhavo ārūpyabhava iti | trayo bhavāḥ | kāmabhavo kāmarāgaḥ | rūpārūpyayor bhavarāga rva na kāmarāgaḥ || tathā coktam Abhidharme || bhavarāgo dvidhātuja iti || tad evaṃ kāmaṃ rāgādibhir atīto 'tikrānto bhavarāgādyatīta ity ucyate |
sa eva bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ | bhavatrayamahāratir iti | tathāpi (?) bhavatraye kāmadhātvādau tatbhūmijānāṃ devaputrāṇām anugrahārthaṃ tatra tatra janmasaṃdarśanāt bhavatrayamahāratir iti ||
śuddhaḥ śubhrābhradhavalaḥ śaraccandrāṃśusuprabhaḥ | bālārkamaṇḍalacchāyo mahārāganakhaprabhaḥ || indranīlāgrasaccīro mahānīlakacāgradhṛk | mahāmaṇimayūkhaśrīr buddhanirvāṇabhūṣaṇa iti || (MNS 9.7-8)	tatra śuddha iti | avidyāmalavigamāt | prajñācakrāntarvartinaḥ sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasyādiyoge vyākhyātāny avaśi + ni padānīti |
na tenābhidhīyante ||
lokadhātuśatākampī ṛddhipādā(!)mahākramaḥ | mahāsmṛtidharas tattvaś catuḥsmṛtisamādhirāḍ iti || (MNS 9.9) tatra lokadhātuśatākampīti | lokadhātunāṃ śatāni lokadhātuśatāni | tāni kampayituṃ śīlaṃ yasya sa lokadhātuśatākampī | etac ca muditāyāṃ bhūmau vyavasthitasya bodhisattvasya ṛddhimāhātmyaṃ darśitaṃ || tathā coktaṃ Candrakīrtipādaiḥ ||
jātaḥ kule bhavati caiṣa tathāgatānāṃ saṃyojanatrayam api kṛtam [=kṣatam] asya sarvaṃ | modaṃ bibharti ca paraṃ sa hi bodhisattva ālokadhātuśatakampanajātasaktir iti ||
ṛddhipādamahākrama iti kāyacittavivekam āśratya (!) vipakṣavirāganirdhasaṃniśrayānabhisaṃskārapravṛttiparyantāvasānāc chandavīryacittamīmāṃsālakṣaṇāś catvāra ṛddhipādāḥ [|] teṣāṃ ṛddhipādānāṃ mahān kramaḥ parākramo yasya sa ṛddhipādamahākramaḥ || mahāsmṛtidhara iti | mahatī smṛtiḥ |
mahāsmṛtiḥ sā ca saṃstute vastuny asaṃpramoṣalakṣaṇā tāṃ dhārayatīti sa tathā || tattva iti | kalpitaparatantrapariniṣpannasvabhāvatvāt | kim atra svabhāvatraye tattvam iṣyate | asac ca nityaṃ sac cāpy atattvataḥ sadasattattvataś ceti | svabhāvatrayam iṣyate | parikalpitalakṣaṇaṃ nityam asad iti |
etat parikalpite svabhāve tattvam avipari[¯]tasvabhāvatvāt | paratantralakṣaṇaṃ sac ca na ca tattvataḥ bhrāntimātratvāt | etat paratantre svabhāve tattvaṃ | pariniṣpannalakṣaṇaṃ sadasattattvataś ceti | etat pariniṣpannasvabhāve tattvam iti tattvayogāt [|] sa eva bhagavān tattva ity ucyate || catuḥsmṛtisamādhirāḍ iti | catvāri smṛtyupasthānāni |
tadyathā kāyasmṛtyupasthānaṃ | nānāvayavasaṃnive + + + ((ma))yaṃ kāyo grāhyādiviviktatālakṣaṇaś ceti smṛter upasthānāṃ | evaṃ vedanāsmṛtyupasthānaṃ | cittasmṛtyupasthānaṃ | dharmasmṛtyupasthānaṃ ca | cā + + + + vīryasamādhismṛtisaṃprajanyayogāt || rājata iti rāṭ || caturṇāṃ smṛtyupasthānānāṃ samādhirāṭ catuḥsmṛtisamādhirāṭ sa eva bhagavān ||
bodhyaṅgakusumāmodas tathāgataguṇodadhiḥ | aṣṭāṅgamārganayavit samyaksambuddhamārgavid iti || (MNS 9.10) tatra bodhyaṅgakusumāmoda iti | anabhisaṃskāravāhibhyo grabhūtebhyaḥ śraddhādibhyo darśanamārgaparyāpannāni dharmadhātugocarāṇi sapta bodhyaṅgāni uktalakṣaṇāni tāny eva kusumāni | bhāvanāmārgaphalahetubhūtatvāt bodhyaṅgakusumānīty ucyante |
teṣām āmodaḥ śrutamayena jñānenādhigatasya | māyāsvapnamarīcigandharvanagarākāśapratibhāsalakṣaṇasya jagatas taddarśanamārge sākṣātkaraṇatvāt mahān āmodo harṣaś cittasya bhavatīti | bodhyaṅgakusumāmoda iti bhaṇyate || tathāgataguṇodadhir iti | tathāgatānāṃ guṇās tathāgataguṇāḥ |
aṣṭādaśāveṇikādayo buddhadharmās ta evodadhayaḥ prabhūtatvād anyeṣāṃ ca guṇaratnānāṃ ākaratvāc ca tathāgataguṇodadhir bhagavān || aṣṭāṅgamārganayavid iti | bhāvanāmārgaprabhāvitāny aṣṭau mārgāṅgāṇi purastāt pratipāditāni | teṣāṃ nayo mārga upāya iti yāvat | taṃ vetti jānātīti aṣṭāṅgamārganayavit || samyaksambuddhamārgavid iti |
samyaksambuddha iti | svayam aparopadeśādiṣṭatvāvabodhāt sanmārgāvabodhāc ca samyaksambuddhasya mārgo dharmadhātus taṃ vettīti samyaksambuddhamārgavit sa eva bhagavān ||
sarvasattvamahāsaṅgo niḥsaṅgo gaganopamaḥ | sarvasattvamanojātaḥ sarvasattvamanojava iti || (MNS 9.11) tatra sarvasattvamahāsaṅga iti | mahāṃś cāsau saṅgaś ca mahāsaṅgaḥ | sarvasattvānāṃ mahāsaṅgaḥ sarvasattvamahāsaṅgaḥ | tatra catvāri saṅgrahavastūni dānaṃ priyavacanam arthacaryā samānārthatā ceti | dānenārthajñaḥ | priyavacanena kālajñaḥ |
arthacaryayā parārthajñaḥ | samānārthatayā dharmajñaḥ | sattvaparāparajñatayā niṣpāditāni saṅgrahavastūni | ebhiḥ saṅgrahavastubhiḥ sarvasattvānātmama <sākṣā>tkarotīti sarvasattvamahāsaṅga ity ucyate | saṃvṛtitaḥ sa eva bhagavān || paramārthatas tu sa eva bhagavān niḥsaṅgaḥ śūnyatāsvabhāvatvāt | ata eva gaganopamaḥ prajñāpāramitāsvabhāvatvāt ||
sarvasattvamanojāta iti | manasi jāto manojātaḥ sarvasattvānāṃ manojātaḥ sarvasattvamanojātaḥ || tathā coktaṃ || tathāgatagarbhāḥ sarvasattvā iti || sarvasattvamanojava iti | manojavas tathāgatavega ity ucyate | sarvasattvānāṃ manojavaḥ sarvasattvamanojavaḥ | sarvasattvacittacaritānuvartitvāt ||
sarvasattvendriyārthajñaḥ sarvasattvamanoharaḥ [|] pañcaskandhārthatattvajñaḥ pañcaskandhaviśuddhadhṛk || (MNS 9.12) tatra sarvasattvendriyārthajña iti | sarvasattvānām indriyāṇi mṛdumadhyādhimātrāṇi teṣāṃ arthaḥ sarvasattvendriyārthas taj jānātīti sarvasattvendriyārthajñaḥ sa eva bhagavān || sarvasattvamanohara iti |
sarvasattvānāṃ manāṃsi cittāni yathābhavyatayā dharmadeśanāmukhena haratīti sa tathā || pañcaskandhārthatattvajña iti | tatra pañca skandhā rūpavedanāsaṃjñāsaṃskāravijñānalakṣaṇāḥ tatra rūpaskandhaś cakṣuḥśrotraghrāṇajihvākāyarūpaśabdagandharasaspraṣṭavyā vijñaptilakṣaṇaḥ | vedanāskandha[...]bhayasvabhāvaḥ |
saṃjñāskandho viṣayanimittodgrahaṇātmakaḥ | saṃskāraskandhaś cittasaṃprayuktaviprayuktarūpaḥ | aṣṭau vijñānakāyā vijñānaskandha iti | ete pañca skandhāḥ teṣām arthaḥ pañcaskandhārthaḥ | sa ca rāśyārthāḥ | tasya tattvaṃ vivekārthāvabodhaḥ | tatra pañcaskandhārthatattvaṃ | ekatvā + grāhanivṛttilakṣaṇaṃ |
tannivṛttir nairātmyaṃ tasmāc cānupadhiśeṣe nirvāṇāvāptir iti | tad evaṃbhūtaṃ pañcaskandhārthatattvaṃ jānātīti pañcaskandhārthatattvajñaḥ | sa eva bhagavān ||
tathā coktaṃ vasubandhupādaiḥ || dharmāṇāṃ pravicayam antareṇa nāsti kleśānāṃ yata upaśāntaye 'bhyupāya ity evamādi || pañcaskandhaviśuddhadhṛg iti | pañca skandhāś ca te viśuddhāś ca pañcaskandhaviśuddhāḥ śīlasamādhiprajñāvimuktivimuktijñānadarśanalakṣaṇāḥ | tān dhārayatīti pañcaskadhaviśuddhadhṛk | sa eva bhagavān ||
sarvaniryāṇakoṭisthaḥ sarvaniryāṇakovidaḥ | sarvaniryāṇamārgasthaḥ sarvaniryāṇadeśakaḥ iti || (MNS 9.13) tatra sarvaniryāṇakoṭistha iti | yāni kānicil laukikanirvāṇāni teṣāṃ koṭir niṣṭhā tatra tiṣṭhatīti sarvaniryāṇakoṭisthaḥ || sarvaniryāṇakovida iti | śrāvakāṇāṃ nirvāṇe kovido nipuṇaḥ sarvaniryāṇakovidaḥ || sarvaniryāṇamārgastha iti |
pratyekabuddhānāṃ nirvāṇamārgasthitaḥ | sarvaniryāṇamārgasthaḥ || sarvaniryāṇadeśaka iti | samyaksaṃbuddhānāṃ nirvāṇaṃ sarvaniryāṇaṃ tasya deśakaḥ prakāśakaḥ | sa eva bhagavān ||
dvādaśāṅgabhavotkhāto dvādaśākāraśuddhadhṛk | catuḥsatyanayākāra aṣṭajñānāvabodhadhṛg iti || (MNS 9.14 = 132) tatra dvādaśāṅgabhavotkhāta iti | dvādaśāṅgāni | tadyathā || avidyāsaṃskāravijñānanāmarūpaṣaḍāyatanasparśavedanātṛṣṇā-upādānabhavajātijarāmaraṇāni || tattvacchādanād avidyāyā yathābhimatadarśanavibandhanāt |
ropaṇāt saṃskārair vijñāne karmavāsanāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāpanāt | nayanāt vijñānenopapattisthānaprāpaṇāt || saṃparigrahāt nāmarūpeṇātmabhāvasya | pūraṇāt ṣaḍāyatanena | triparicchedāt sparśena | upabhogād vedanayā | karṣaṇāc ca tṛṣṇayā karmavikṣiptasya punar bhavasya nibandhanāt upādānair vijñānasyopapattyanukūleṣu kāmādiṣu |
ābhimukhyāt bhavena kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ punarbhave vipākadānāyābhimukhīkaraṇāt | duḥkhanāt | jātyā jarāmaraṇena ca tad ebhir dvādaśāṅgaiḥ pratilomavibhavair bhavaḥ pañcopādānalakṣaṇa utkhāta unmūlito yena sa dvādaśāṅgabhavotkhāta ity ucyate ||
tathā coktaṃ Madhyāntavibhāge || cchādanād ropaṇāc caiva nayanāt samparigrahāt | pūraṇāt triparicchedād upabhogāc ca karṣaṇāt || nibandhanād ābhimukhyād duḥkhanāt kliśyate jagat ||
dvādaśākāraśuddhadhṛg iti | dvādaśākārāṇi dvādaśāyatanāni tāni viśuddhāni | grāhyagrāhakākāraviviktatvāt tāni dhārayatīti dvādaśākāraśuddhadhṛk || catuḥsatyanayākāra iti | duḥkhasamudayanirodhamārgākhyāni catur āryasatyāni | teṣāṃ catuḥsatyānāṃ nayo mārgaḥ sa evākāra ākṛtir yasya sa catuḥsatyanayākāro bhagavān || aṣṭajñānāvabodhadhṛg iti |
tatrāṣṭa jñānāni yaduta dharmajñānam anvayajñānaṃ duḥkhajñānaṃ samudayajñānaṃ nirodhajñānaṃ mārgajñānaṃ kṣayajñānam anutpādajñānam iti | teṣām avabodhaḥ sākṣātkaraṇaṃ taṃ dhārayatīti | aṣṭajñānāvabodhadhṛk | sa eva bhagavān ||
dvādaśākārasatyārthaḥ ṣoḍaśākāratattvavit | viṃśatyākārasaṃbodhir vibuddhaḥ sarvavit para iti || (MNS 9.15 = 133) tatra dvādaśākārasatyārtha iti | satyānām arthaḥ satyārthaḥ | dvādaśākāraiḥ pravibhajya triparivarta dvādaśākaraṃ dharmacakraṃ yaduta anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyata iti | duḥkhasatyasya || hetutaḥ samudayataḥ prabhavata iti |
samudayasatyasya || nirodhataḥ śāntataḥ praṇītata iti | nirodhasatyasya || mārgato nyāyataḥ pratipattitaś ceti | mārgasatyasya || tad evaṃbhūtaṃ yathābhavyatatyā dvādaśākārasatyārthadharmacakrapravartanād asau bhagavān dvādaśākāra ity ucyate ||
śoḍaśākāratattvavid iti | tatra kāmāvacaraṃ duḥkhasatyam anityato duḥkhataḥ śūnyato 'nātmataś ca manasikurvato duḥkhe dharmajñānakṣāntiḥ duḥkhe dharmajñānaṃ cotpadyate | tābhyāṃ daśāpy ete | kāmāvacarāḥ kleśāḥ prahīyante | rūpārūpyāvacaraduḥkhasatyaṃ tathaiva manasikurvato duḥkhe 'nvayajñānakṣāntiḥ duḥkhe 'nvayajñānaṃ cotpadyate |
tābhyāṃ ta eva pratigha(?)varjitā navarūpāvacarānavacārūpyāvacarā ity aṣṭādaśa prahīyante | ity evaṃ traidhātukaduḥkhasatyābhisamayena caturbhir dharmānvayajñānakṣāntikṣaṇair aṣṭāviṃśatir anuśayā duḥkhadarśanāt prahīyante |
eṣām eva daśānām anuśayānāṃ satkāyadṛṣṭim antagrāhadṛṣṭiṃ śīlavrataparāmarṣam uktānye saptānuśayāḥ kāmadhātau kāmāvacaraṃ samudayasatyaṃ hetutaḥ samudayataḥ prabhavataḥ pratyayataś ca manasikurvataḥ | ta eva samudaye dharmajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ prahīyante |
ta eva pratighavivarjitā dhātudvaye dvādaśānuśayā rūpārūpyāvacarāḥ samudayasatyadarśanāt samudaye 'nvayajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ prahīyanta ity ekonaviṃśatir anuśayāḥ samudayasatyadarśanāt prahīyante |
tathā kāmāvacaraṃ duḥkhanirodhasatyaṃ nirodhaśāntapraṇītaṃ niḥsaraṇākāraiś caturbhir manasikurvataḥ | samudayoktās ta eva saptānuśayā nirodhe dharmajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ prahīyante | evam anvayajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ rūpārūpyāvacarā dvādaśānuśayāḥ prahīyante | ety evam ekānna(!)viṃśatir anuśayās traidhātukaduḥkhanirodhasatyadarśanāt prahīyante |
tathā kāmāvacaraduḥkhanirodhagāmimārgasatyaṃ mārganyāyapratipannair yāṇikākāraiś (!) caturbhir manasikurvataḥ | mārge dharmajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ pūrvokteṣv evaṃ saptasu śīlavrataparāmarṣam adhikam aṣṭamaṃ prakṣipyāṣṭau mārgasatyaṃ darśanena prahīyante | mārge 'nvayajñānakṣāntijñānābhyāṃ rūpārūpyāvacarāś caturdaśā mārgadarśanaprahātavyāḥ |

paramārthaviśuddhaśrīś trailokyasubhago mahān | sarvasaṃpatkaraḥ śrīmān mañjuśrīḥ śrīmatāṃ vara iti || (MNS 10.15 = 157) tatra paramārthena dharmadhātunā viśuddhā śrīr yasyāsau paramārthaviśuddhaśrīḥ prajñopāyātmakatvāt || ata eva trailokyasubhago (!) iti | trailokye eka eva subhagaḥ | sarvasattvārthakaraṇāt || tathā coktaṃ śrīsamvaratantre ||
subhago bhagavān vajrī sarvasattvārthakaraṇāt | durbhagāḥ śrāvakāḥ sarve| atyantaparinirvṛtā iti || mahān iti [|] sarvavyāpakatvāt || sarvasampatkara iti | sarvasampadādhārabhūtatvāt || śrīmān iti | bhāgyasampannatvāt || mañjuśrīr iti | saṃjñābhidhānaṃ || śrīmatāṃ vara iti | tadvatāṃ śreṣṭha ity arthaḥ ||
idam idānīṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhājñānasvabhāvena sa eva bhagavān mañjuśrīḥ sarvatathāgatajñānasattvaḥ sarvatathāgatasarvamudrāsamadhigataviśvakāryakaraṇād aśeṣasarvasattvadhātusarvāśāparipūrakaḥ sarvadaśadiganantāparyantalokadhātūn gatvā nāmamantrākṣarapadānāṃ pañcānavatīnāṃ kulabhedena ekaikasya ekonaviṃśatiṃ ekonaviṃśatiṃ pañcadhā pravibhajya taiḥ
sarvasattvānāṃ kauśīdyam apanīya kuśalapakṣeṣu saṃsthāpya svavimokṣamukhena tatprabhayā ca sarvatathāgataparṣadam avabhāsya punar āgatya tāni coktalakṣair nāmamantrākṣarapadāni kṛtvā vajradhātumahāmaṇḍalasya bahiḥ pañcamacakre yathānukramato vyavasthitaḥ punar api yathoktarūpatrayam ātmānam ālambya mantramukhacaryācārī kulaputro vā kuladuhitā vā
āryanāmasaṅgītiṃ paṭhan pūrvoktān guṇān prāpnotīty āmnāyaḥ ||   ||
āryanāmasaṅgītiṭīkāyāṃ nāmamantrārthāvalokinyāṃ kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānastutyadhikāro daśamaḥ ||   || gāthā pañcadaśa nāmamantrapadāni pañcānavatir aṅkato 'pi || 95 ||
adhunā mahāvairocanādayaḥ pañca tathāgatāḥ pañcajñānātmikābhir gāthābhir bhagavantaṃ jñānamūrtiṃ vajradhātumahāmaṇḍalāntarvartinaṃ saṃstuvanti sma || tadyathā | namas te varadetyādi | ādarśamukhena || buddharāgetyādi | <dharmadhātujñānasvabhāvena || buddhasmitetyādi | pratyavekṣaṇājñānadvāreṇa || abhavodbhavetyādi | samatājñānātmanā ||
māyājāletyādi |> kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānasvabhāvena | iti ||   ||
āryanāmasaṅgītiṭīkāyāṃ nāmamantrārthāvalokinyām upasaṃhārastutyadhikāra ekādaśatamaḥ parisamāptaḥ || gāthāḥ pañca ||   ||
athedānīṃ bhagavān śākyamunir vajradharam āmantrya tenaivādhyeṣitatvāt tasyaivāmantraṇaṃ kṛtvā sānuśaṃsāṃ nāmasaṅgītim adhikṛtyāha || śraddhāvatāṃ pravṛttihetoḥ || iyam asau vajrapāṇe vajradhareti | iyam asau nāmasaṅgītiḥ | he vajrapāṇe | idam abhiṣekadvāreṇa || vajradhareti jñānadvāreṇa |
ubhayaguṇatvena saṅgītikārakatvenādhikṛtatvād bhagavato jñānamūrteḥ sarvatathāgatajñānakāyasya mañjuśrījñānasattvasya sambandhinī nāmasaṅgītiḥ |
kiṃviśiṣṭā āveṇikapariśuddhā | tatra veṇir miśrībhāvaḥ kleśaiḥ saha | na veṇir aveṇiḥ | śuddhasvabhāvas tatra bhava āveṇikas tena pariśuddhā yā nāmasaṅgītiḥ sā mayā deśiteti vakṣyamāṇena sambandhaḥ [|] kimartham ity āha [|] bhavānuttaraprītiprasādamahaudbilyasaṃjananārtham iti |
tatrānuttaraprītir harṣaḥ prasādaḥ śraddhā sā ca karmaphalasatyaratneṣv abhisampratyayaḥ | mahaudbilyaṃ vīryaṃ tac ca kuśalapra + tyutsāhaḥ |
eṣāṃ vajrapāṇe bhavasaṃjananārtham utpādanārthaṃ kāyavāṅmanoguhyapariśuddhyai kāyavāṅmanasāṃ guhyaṃ bodhicittavajrasvabhāvam | tasya pariśuddhyarthaṃ | aparipūrṇāpariśuddhabhūmipāramitāpuṇyajñānasaṃbhāraparipūriparipariśuddhyai aparipūrṇā bhūmayaḥ | apariśuddhāś ca pāramitāḥ | aparipūrṇāpariśuddhabhūmipāramitāḥ |
tāsāṃ puṇyajñānasambhāras tasya paripūripariśuddhyarthaṃ || anadhigatānuttarārthasyādhigamāya | anadhigataś cāsāv anuttarārthaś ca | anadhigatānuttarārthas tasyādhigamārtham |
aprāptasya buddhadharmādeḥ prāptyarthaṃ yāvat sarvatathāgatasadharmanetrīsandhāraṇārthaṃ ceti | yāvacchabdaḥ kramāpekṣī | sarvatathāgatasaddharmanetrī prajñāpāramitā | tasyā advayajñānasvarūpeṇa samyag dhāraṇārthaṃ ca || mayā deśiteti | bodhicittavajrasvabhāvena | samprakāśiteti | supariśuddhadharmadhātujñānamukhena || vivṛteti |
ādarśajñānarūpeṇa || vibhajiteti | samatājñānamukhena || uttānīkṛteti | pratyavekṣaṇājñānātmanā || adhiṣṭhiteti | kṛtyānuṣṭhānajñānadvāreṇa || iyaṃ nāma saṅgītir he vajrapāṇe vajradhara bhavasantāne citte | sarvamantradharmatādhiṣṭhāneneti | sarvamantrāṇāṃ dharmatā śūnyatā tasyā adhiṣṭhānaṃ sthirīkaraṇaṃ tenādhiṣṭhāneneti ||
prathamacakrasyeyam anuśaṃsā ekādaśapadāni aṅkato 'pi || 11 || o ||
atha dvitīyacakrasyānuśaṃsām adhikṛtyāha || punar aparam iyaṃ vajrapāṇe vajradharanāmasaṅgītir iti | supariśuddhaparyavadātasarvajñajñānakāyavāṅmanoguhyabhūteti || suṣṭhu pariśuddhaṃ parisamantād avadātaṃ paryavadātaṃ supariśuddhaparyavadātaṃ ca tat sarvajñajñānaṃ ca |
supariśuddhaparyavadātasarvajñajñānaṃ tasya kāyavāṅmanāṃsi teṣāṃ guhyabhūtā aprakāśyarūpatvāt | sarvatathāgatānāṃ buddhabodhir iti | buddhānāṃ bodhiḥ | buddhabodhiḥ na śrāvakabodhir ity arthaḥ ||
samyaksambuddhānām abhisamaya iti | abhisamayo jñānaṃ | sarvajñajñānaṃ | sarvatathāgatānām anuttara iti na vidyate uttaro 'smād ity anuttaraḥ || dharmadhātugatiḥ sugatānām iti | śūnyatāsvabhāvatvāt || sarvamārabalaparājayo jinānām iti | caturmārabalair aparājitatvāt || daśabalabalitā sarvadaśabalānām iti | daśa balāni uktalakṣaṇāni |
tāni vidyante yasyā nāmasaṅgīteḥ | sā daśabalabalinī | tasyā bhāvo daśabalabalitā || sarvajñatā sarvajñajñānānām iti | sarvaṃ jānātīti sarvajñas tasya bhāvaḥ sarvajñatā |
āgamaḥ sarvadharmāṇām iti (!) | parasmād āgacchatīty āgamaḥ | sa ca dviḥprakāraḥ | āgamalakṣaṇo 'dhigamalakṣaṇaś ca || samudāgamaḥ sarvabuddhānām iti | śrāvakādīnāṃ sarvabuddhānāṃ śrāvakādisvarūpeṇa sākṣātkaraṇāt || vimalasupariśuddhapuṇyajñānasambhāraparipūriḥ sarvamahābodhisattvānām iti || vimalaś cāsau supariśuddhavimalasupariśuddhaḥ |
sa cāsau puṇyajñānasambhāraś ca | vimalasupariśuddhapuṇyajñānasambhāraḥ | tasya paripūrir yasyāṃ nāmasaṅgītau | sā tathoktā ||
prasūtiḥ sarvaśrāvakapratyekabuddhānām iti | prasūtir iti | teṣām utpattisthānatvāt | kṣetraṃ sarvadevamanuṣyāṇāṃ sampatter iti | kṣetram iti | sarvasampadām ākaratvāt || pratiṣṭhā mahāyānasyeti | uktalakṣaṇasya mahāyānasya sthitirūpatvāt || sambhavo bodhisattvasya caryāyā iti | sambhava ity utpattisthānatvāt | niṣṭhā samyagāryamārgasyeti |
niṣṭhāvasānaṃ ||
nikaṣo vimuktīnām iti [|] nikaṣaḥ parīkṣāsthānam | yathā suvarṇasya nikaṣopalaḥ || utpattir niryāṇamārgasyeti | nirvāṇopāyasya hetubhūtatvād asyāḥ anucchedas tathāgatavaṃśasyeti | anuccheda ity atyanucchedaḥ (?) | tathāgatakṛtyakaraṇāt || pravṛddhir mahābodhisattvakulagotrasyeti |
ata eva prakarṣeṇa vṛddhir bhavati nigrahaḥ sarvaparapravādinām iti | nigṛhyate 'neneti nigrahaḥ ||
tathā coktaṃ śrīsamvare || kvacic ca paratīrthyānāṃ saha dharmeṇa nigraha iti ||
vidhvaṃsanaṃ sarvatīrthikānām iti | vidhvaṃsanam iti | svasya māhātmyapradarśanamukhena nirākaraṇaṃ | yathā devāvatāre | parājayaś caturmārabalacamūsenāyā iti | balam iti | caturaṅgabalaṃ | camū vyūharacanā | senā samudāyaḥ | tataś cāyam artho bhavati | caturṇāṃ mārāṇāṃ balacamūsenāyāḥ parājayo nirākaraṇaṃ yan nāmasaṅgītiḥ |
saṅgrahaḥ sarvasattvānām iti | saṅgraha iti | dānapriyavacanārthacaryāsamānārthatālakṣaṇaḥ | tena saṃgṛhyata iti | saṅgrahaḥ | āryamārgaparipākaḥ sarvaniryāṇayāyinām iti || āryamārgaparipākaḥ | āryāṣṭāṅgamārgasya paripakvatā ||
samādhiś caturbrahmavihāravihāriṇām iti | samādhirūpaparīkṣite vastuni cittasyaikāgratā sā ca maitrīkaruṇāmuditopekṣāṇāṃ || dhyānam ekāgracittānām iti | dhyānaṃ vitarkavicāraprītisukhākhyaṃ || yogaḥ kāyavāṅmano[']bhiyuktānām iti | yoga iti jñānamātrasamāpattiḥ || visaṃyogaḥ sarvasaṃyojanānām iti | visaṃyoga iti | rahitā navabhyaḥ saṃyojanebhyaḥ |
nava saṃyojanāni | anunayasaṃyojanaṃ | pratighasaṃyojanaṃ | mānasaṃyojanaṃ | avidyāsaṃyojanaṃ | dṛṣṭasaṃyojanaṃ | parāmarṣasaṃyojanaṃ | vicikitsāsaṃyojanaṃ | īrṣyāsaṃyojanaṃ | mātsaryasaṃyojanam iti || prahāṇaṃ sarvakleśopakleśānām iti | prahāṇam iti parityāgaḥ | rāgādīnāṃ krodhādīnāṃ ca ||
vyupaśamaḥ sarvāvaraṇānām iti | jñeyakleśakarmavipākanīvaraṇānām atyantābhāvaḥ || vimuktiḥ sarvabandhanānām iti | rāgadveṣamohabandhanānāṃ vimuktihetutvāt || mokṣaḥ sarvopadhīnām iti | sopadhinirupadhiśeṣalakṣaṇānāṃ mokṣāṇāṃ tatsvabhāvatvāt || śāntiḥ sarvacittopaplavānām iti | śamanaṃ śāntiḥ śāntatāvihāritvāt || ākaraḥ sarvasampattīnām iti |

pithanaṃ (pidhanaṃ?) sarvāpāyadvārāṇām iti | narakapretatiraścām apāyadvārāṇāṃ ḍhakkanatvāt | satpatho vimuktipurasyeti sac cāsau panthāś ca satpathaḥ | apravṛttiḥ saṃsāracakrasyeti | pravartanaṃ pravṛttiḥ [|] na pravṛtthir apravṛtthiḥ | pravartanaṃ dharmacakrasyeti | yathābhavyatayā dharmacakrapravartanāt ||
ucchritacchatradhvajapatākās tathāgataśāsanasyeti | bāhyaśrāvakādīnāṃ puṇyam abhibhūya ucchritacchatradhvajapatākeva nāmasaṅgītiḥ || adhiṣṭhānaṃ sarvadharmadeśanāyā iti | adhiṣṭhānam iti sthirīkaraṇaṃ | kṣiprasiddhir mantramukhacaryācāriṇāṃ bodhisattvānām iti || kṣiprasiddhir iti | puraścaraṇanirapekṣatayā śīghrasiddhiḥ ||
bhāvanādhigamaḥ prajñāpāramitābhiyuktānām iti | bhāvanāyāḥ punaḥ punar abhyāsasyādhigamaḥ sākṣātkaraṇam || śūnyatāprativedhyo 'dvayaprativedhabhāvanābhiyuktānām iti | prativedho 'vagamaḥ | śūnyatāyāḥ prativedhaḥ śūnyatāprativedhaḥ | niṣpattiḥ sarvapāramitāsambhārasyeti | niṣpattir iti | sampādanaṃ ||
